# Career routes and earnings evidence

Research snapshot: 17 October 2026. India; 15 requested specialties.

India: 15 requested postgraduate branches; dated evidence and editorial decision support, not individual career or financial guarantees.

## How to read salary evidence

- Each row is one dated policy, institutional notice or advertised role, not an all-India salary survey. Offers and actual payments have not been independently confirmed.
- All amounts are INR. Monthly pay, annual remuneration, pay-matrix basic, consolidated pay and training stipends retain their original units and basis. Annual divided by 12 is only a monthly equivalent, never take-home pay.
- Government basic pay excludes applicable NPA/DA/HRA and deductions unless the source explicitly states otherwise. A pay-band maximum is not an entry salary. Stipends are paid during training, not after qualification.
- Tier 1 is an editorial comparison group: Delhi-NCR, Mumbai metro, Bengaluru, Chennai, Hyderabad, Kolkata, Pune and Ahmedabad. Tier 2 names secondary urban centres explicitly; smaller towns, districts, multiple locations and national policies are separately labelled. These are not official HRA X/Y/Z categories.
- Missing pay remains null, never zero. Negotiable roles and contradictory portal fields are labelled. A recruiter listing does not establish a funded vacancy, current availability, a named corporate employer or average realised pay.
- Salary stages follow explicit qualifications and experience in the source. Fresh MD, fellow, DM/DrNB trainee and post-SS consultant are different stages; no fixed fellowship premium or guaranteed increment is inferred.
- Private practice receipts, hospital billings and procedure fees are revenue, not personal earnings. Rent, staff, equipment, finance, consumables, taxes, collection delays and referral ethics matter.
- Corporate-chain salary tables by branch, city and experience are not consistently public. Named-chain examples are shown where available; unidentified clients and missing offers remain evidence gaps. No salary league table is constructed from this convenience sample.
- Saturation is assessed as a local employment and referral problem. Seat counts, portal listing counts and podcast anecdotes cannot measure unemployment or prove a national surplus of specialists.

## NEET-SS seat baseline

NEET-SS 2025 — Initial final Round 1 DM/MCh and DrNB matrices, published 11 March 2026.

The official pages retrieved on 17 October 2026 linked NEET-SS 2025 and SS 2025 counselling. A NEET-SS 2026 bulletin/complete matrix was not located; no 2026 eligibility or seat counts are presented.

The complete initial MCC matrices contain 7,579 seats (DM/MCh 4,312; DrNB 3,267). Parent-branch totals overlap. Counts exclude INI-SS, FNB, later additions/withdrawals and retained seats not in the matrices. They are not current vacancies.

| Parent branch | Destination subjects | Award + subject options | Listed eligible seats |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| ENT / Otorhinolaryngology | 5 | 9 | 1,248 |
| Palliative Medicine | 1 | 2 | 13 |
| Pharmacology | 2 | 3 | 25 |
| Pathology | 3 | 5 | 156 |
| Geriatric Medicine | 1 | 2 | 13 |
| Family Medicine | 1 | 2 | 13 |
| General Medicine | 13 | 24 | 4,184 |
| Paediatrics | 20 | 34 | 3,969 |
| Biochemistry | 2 | 4 | 102 |
| Ophthalmology | 1 | 2 | 13 |
| Radio-diagnosis / Radiology | 3 | 5 | 84 |
| Dermatology, Venereology & Leprosy | 1 | 2 | 13 |
| Nuclear Medicine | 1 | 2 | 13 |
| Radiation Oncology | 2 | 4 | 373 |
| General Surgery | 14 | 23 | 2,677 |

## ENT / Otorhinolaryngology

MS/DNB ENT can support broad clinical and surgical ENT practice, or a more focused ear, nose, voice, pediatric or head-and-neck path. NEET-SS, NBEMS FNB and institutional fellowships have separate entry and recognition rules.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **General ENT consultant / surgeon** — Hospital OPD, equipped operating service, trust or private practice. Diagnose and manage common ENT disease, emergencies and rehabilitation within demonstrated competence, registration and facility privileges. An MS/DNB label alone is not permission for every skull-base or oncologic operation. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Otorhinolaryngology curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Otorhinolaryngology&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Senior resident / supervised clinical post** — Teaching or service hospital. Consolidate graded operating and emergency skills, documentation and communication; inspect actual case access and backup rather than a degree label. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Otorhinolaryngology curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Otorhinolaryngology&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Teaching, audit and clinical research** — Academic department or clinical service. Curriculum objectives include teaching, research methods, community deafness programmes and counselling. Faculty appointment additionally requires the applicable current recruitment/teacher-eligibility criteria. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Otorhinolaryngology curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Otorhinolaryngology&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)

### Superspecialty route details

- **MCh Head and Neck Surgery** (Verified feeder). NEET-SS 2025 followed by applicable counselling; seat availability must be checked in that admission cycle. MS/DNB Otorhinolaryngology is explicitly listed as a feeder in NEET-SS 2025 Annexure A/B. Eligibility is distinct from current seat availability. Duration: Confirm the current admitting-institute prospectus for this post-PG MCh programme; the DrNB duration source does not establish its duration.. MS/DNB Otorhinolaryngology is explicitly listed as a feeder in NEET-SS 2025 Annexure A/B. Eligibility is distinct from current seat availability. Duration: DrNB: three years after MD/MS/DNB under the official programme overview. For MCh, confirm the current admitting-institute prospectus.. Sources: [NEET-SS 2025 ENT feeder table](https://nbe.edu.in/IB/NEET-SS%202025%20information%20bulletin.pdf) (PDF page(s) 113, 119); [NBEMS DrNB Super Specialty programme duration and entry routes](https://natboard.edu.in/dnbsuper) (DrNB overview / Admission and Registration)

### Fellowships and focused training

FNB and institutional awards are separate routes; verify current registration, recognition, privileges, intake, fees and stipend. This is an evidenced selection, not an exhaustive national fellowship register.

- **Head and Neck Oncology — NBEMS-accredited institutions**. FNB. Entry: FET 2025 and NBEMS centralized fellowship counselling. Eligibility: MS/DNB Otorhinolaryngology explicitly listed; other qualifying routes separately listed in Annexure B.. Duration: 2 years; confirm specialty curriculum and current allotment notice. FNB is a separate NBEMS qualification, not DM/MCh/DrNB. The cited Gazette annexure states FNB is not a recognized medical qualification for teaching-faculty purposes; verify the current exact specialty award and rules. Sources: [FET 2025 Indian-candidate feeder annexure](https://www.nbe.edu.in/IB/FET%202025%20information%20bulletin%2012.01.2026_v2.pdf) (PDF page(s) 130, 131); [FNB duration, recognition and teaching-faculty limitation](https://www.nbe.edu.in/IB/FET%202025%20information%20bulletin%2012.01.2026_v2.pdf) (PDF page(s) 122, 124)

### Other pathways

- **Public-health hearing and rehabilitation work**. ENT training includes prevention/control of deafness and rehabilitative care. This supports investigating service or programme roles, not assuming an audiology qualification or an advertised funded vacancy. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Otorhinolaryngology curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Otorhinolaryngology&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)

### Salary and stipend observations

- **Associate Professor (regular/deputation pay scale), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 138,300–209,200 per month; Pay-matrix basic. Stage: Postgraduate academic career: Associate Professor. Qualification: Recognised MD/MS or accepted equivalent in the concerned broad specialty; relevant DM/MCh/equivalent additionally required for superspecialty posts.. Experience: Six years of recognised teaching/research after the qualifying MD/MS/equivalent for broad specialties; three years after a recognised three-year DM/MCh for superspecialty appointments, with other historical routes separately specified.. Level13-A1+ monthly BASIC scale; applicable allowances and NPA are separate and have not been calculated. Scale maximum is a progression endpoint, not an advertised fresh-graduate package or guaranteed offer. Mapped specialties have a nonzero vacancy at this rank in the dated advertisement; category, selection and full eligibility still apply. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli faculty recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/FACULTY/2026/467](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C118) (pp2-6 qualifications/experience; p7 pay tables; pp8-9 department/rank vacancies; p15 contractual conditions)

- **DM/MCh trainee, year 1, Tata Memorial Centre / Tata Memorial Hospital, Mumbai, Maharashtra (Tier 1)**: INR 133,623–133,623 per month; Training stipend. Stage: Super-specialty training year 1. Qualification: Admission to an eligible TMC DM/MCh course through NEET-SS and MCC counselling; parent eligibility is destination-specific.. Experience: Completed eligible postgraduate feeder qualification; this payment is during further training.. 2025 admission guidelines monthly stipend, corroborated by the signed 7 September 2026 FY2026-27 disclosure at the same amounts; not post-training consultant income or evidence of a stipend increase. The website link label says FY2025-26; the scanned linked document explicitly says FY2026-2027. Its date/heading govern this source description. Parent mapping is limited to actual TMC DM/MCh destinations in the initial matrix and eligible feeders; it does not imply every course is open to every mapped parent. ### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

A universal saturated/not-saturated label is not established for ENT. Assess the actual paid role and catchment.

**Evidence limits:** Official curricula and admission bulletins establish skills and routes, not current vacancy rates, local competitor capacity, fee realization or net practice income. No representative specialty-by-city workforce dataset was established in this review. More seats or disease burden alone do not prove funded demand.

- Compare funded general ENT posts with the number and operating capacity of local consultants, rather than counting hospital names.
- Ask for personal supervised ear/endoscopic/airway case logs, anesthesia cover and emergency referral arrangements.
- Check whether the proposed voice, implant or skull-base niche has referrals, equipment, rehabilitation and multidisciplinary colleagues.
- Model consultation receipts and operation fees separately from hospital bills, equipment costs and owner profit.
- A strong broad ENT practice can be an endpoint; a superspecialty is a separate career choice, not proof of higher earnings.
- NEET-SS 2025 includes ENT as a feeder to several surgical SS paths; do not expand that list to every MCh programme.
- For procedural fellowships, insist on graded operating, complication management, logs and current privileges, not observation alone.

Sources: [NBEMS DNB Otorhinolaryngology curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Otorhinolaryngology&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5); [CMC 2026-2027 ENT and Head & Neck fellowships](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 34, 35)

**Open evidence gaps**

- Current vacancies and representative salary/owner-profit distributions were not established by these route documents.
- Institutional fellowship availability and selection dates need their current course-specific bulletin.

### Editorial career planning

A reasonable shortlist if you enjoy both outpatient medicine and fine surgical work. I would prioritise a department that demonstrably graduates safe independent general ENT surgeons before paying for a narrower label.

- **After MS:** Supervised surgical consolidation or a suitably supported general ENT post; compare actual operating access.
- **Focused training / SS:** Compare documented institutional fellowships with the separately verified NEET-SS destinations. ENT is not a feeder for every surgical SS.
- **Established practice:** Evaluate mixed OPD and operating income, referral dependence and the time needed to build case volume.

- Independent common-case operating competence, with safe anaesthesia and emergency support.
- A local referral network and a coherent focus such as otology, rhinology or head-and-neck work, matched to actual case demand.
- Clinic ownership can add business opportunity and capital risk; consultation and surgery revenue are not personal salary.
- Ask how many common operations you personally perform by final year.
- Verify the degree and experience required by the actual employer; some portal ENT ads incorrectly label qualifications.

## Palliative Medicine

MD/DNB Palliative Medicine is a clinical specialty endpoint in its own right: symptom control, communication and interdisciplinary care across cancer and non-cancer serious illness. Further courses should add a defined skill rather than duplicate basic palliative training.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **Specialist palliative-care clinician** — Outpatient clinic, hospital ward, consultation-liaison service, hospice or home-care team. The official curriculum explicitly covers all these settings, supportive care, concurrent illness, complex decisions and end-of-life care. Safe prescribing, referral and local legal/service requirements still apply. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Palliative Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Palliative Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 8)
- **Interdisciplinary service clinician / team lead** — Cancer centre, non-oncology serious-illness service or community/hospice programme. Coordinate symptom, psychosocial, spiritual and family support with other professionals; service design and management are part of the curriculum, not evidence of funded posts everywhere. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Palliative Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Palliative Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 8)
- **Clinical teacher, audit or research clinician** — Academic department or palliative service. Mentor junior doctors and conduct audits or research; statutory faculty appointment requires the current recognized qualification and recruitment criteria. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Palliative Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Palliative Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 8)

### Verified NEET-SS destination seats

| Award | Specialty | Seats | Entry / feeder notes |
|---|---|---:|---|
| DM | Medical Genetics | 9 | NEET-SS: Medical Group; MCC SS counselling; Eligibility and seat availability are different; this is the dated initial matrix, not an admission guarantee. Any recognised MD/MS/DNB broad specialty is eligible; Medical Group question paper is required. This does not open the other Medical Group destinations. |
| DrNB | Medical Genetics | 4 | NEET-SS: Medical Group; MCC SS counselling; Eligibility and seat availability are different; this is the dated initial matrix, not an admission guarantee. Any recognised MD/MS/DNB broad specialty is eligible; Medical Group question paper is required. This does not open the other Medical Group destinations. |

### Superspecialty route details

- **DM / DrNB Medical Genetics** (Verified feeder). NEET-SS 2025 followed by applicable counselling; seat availability must be checked in that admission cycle. MD/DNB Palliative Medicine is covered by the bulletin's Any MD/MS/DNB Broad Specialty feeder. This does not grant entry to other General Medicine routes. Duration: DrNB: three years after MD/MS/DNB under the official programme overview. For DM, confirm the current admitting-institute prospectus.. Sources: [NEET-SS 2025 Medical Genetics any-broad-specialty feeder](https://nbe.edu.in/IB/NEET-SS%202025%20information%20bulletin.pdf) (PDF page(s) 116, 118); [NBEMS DrNB Super Specialty programme duration and entry routes](https://natboard.edu.in/dnbsuper) (DrNB overview / Admission and Registration)

### Fellowships and focused training

FNB and institutional awards are separate routes; verify current registration, recognition, privileges, intake, fees and stipend. This is an evidenced selection, not an exhaustive national fellowship register.

- **Pain Medicine — AIIMS New Delhi**. Institutional Fellowship Programme. Entry: AIIMS fellowship entrance/selection under July 2025 prospectus; verify whether a corresponding 2026/current intake is advertised. Eligibility: MD Palliative Medicine explicitly accepted only with three years post-PG teaching experience as SR/equivalent at an eligible recognized institution; do not assume fresh-MD or DNB equivalence.. Duration: 1 year. Historical programme verified for July 2025, not a confirmed 2026 vacancy. It is an institutional fellowship, not FNB Pain Medicine or DM. Sources: [AIIMS July 2025 Fellowship in Pain Medicine requirements](https://docs.aiimsexams.ac.in/sites/prospectus-FP-July%202025-v3.pdf) (PDF page(s) 7)
- **Clinical Genetics — CMC Vellore**. Post-doctoral institutional fellowship. Entry: Institutional fellowship selection; read the 2026-2027 prospectus with the programme admission bulletin. Eligibility: MD/DNB in any subject is the listed feeder; obtain written confirmation for the exact qualification and intake.. Duration: 2 years. Institutional skills-development award; not equivalent to or a replacement for an NMC-recognized DM/MCh/DrNB. Verify current admission bulletin, assessment and clinical privileges. Sources: [CMC 2026-2027 physician, family and geriatric fellowship feeders](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 33, 34, 35); [CMC fellowship recognition and full-time training restrictions](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 36)
- **Patient Safety including Infection Control — SGPGIMS Lucknow**. PDCC. Entry: SGPGI 2026 institutional entrance/selection. Eligibility: MD/DNB Hospital Administration / Microbiology / Any Broad Specialty. The printed qualification row says MD/DNB; an MS-only holder should obtain written eligibility confirmation rather than assume equivalence.. Duration: 1 year under the PDCC duration rule. Institutional certificate focused on patient safety; not an organ-specialty DM/MCh/DrNB. Obtain written confirmation of the exact feeder, current recognition and clinical or academic portability. Sources: [SGPGI 2026 Patient Safety including Infection Control eligibility](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF page 6, programme and essential-qualification table); [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF admission and SR eligibility brochure](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF page(s) 5, 6, 7, 16)

### Other pathways

- **Teaching, service development and quality improvement**. The palliative curriculum explicitly includes teaching, research, team leadership and management of a palliative service. These are clinical-development directions, not separate automatically recognized specialist qualifications. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Palliative Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Palliative Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 8)
- **Palliative certificate and similarly named fellowship boundaries**. CMC describes its certificate as up-skilling that does not make the holder a palliative specialist. FET 2025 Pain Medicine requires Anaesthesia; SGPGI 2026 Pain and Palliative Care PDAF requires Anaesthesiology and Palliative Oncology PDCC requires Radiotherapy. None is inferred as a direct MD Palliative Medicine route from the course title. Sources: [CMC 2026-2027 palliative certificate scope limitation](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page 32, Certificate Course in Palliative Medicine); [FET 2025 Indian-candidate feeder annexure](https://www.nbe.edu.in/IB/FET%202025%20information%20bulletin%2012.01.2026_v2.pdf) (PDF page(s) 130, 131); [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF admission and SR eligibility brochure](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF page(s) 5, 6, 7, 16)

### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

A universal saturated/not-saturated label is not established for palliative medicine. Assess the actual paid role and catchment.

**Evidence limits:** Official curricula and admission bulletins establish skills and routes, not current vacancy rates, local competitor capacity, fee realization or net practice income. No representative specialty-by-city workforce dataset was established in this review. More seats or disease burden alone do not prove funded demand.

- Identify which hospitals or hospice/home-care organisations have funded specialist posts and who pays for the service.
- Check referral integration with oncology and non-cancer services, pharmacy access, nursing/social-work support and out-of-hours cover.
- Separate unmet patient need from an employer budget, reimbursement mechanism and secure salary contract.
- Inspect whether a proposed pain course teaches procedures suitable for your background and grants supervised clinical access.
- Medical Genetics is the verified NEET-SS option from the any-broad-specialty rule; general medicine organ-specialty eligibility is not inferred.
- FET 2025 Pain Medicine requires MD/DNB Anaesthesia; it must not be advertised as a direct palliative-MD option.
- SGPGI 2026 Pain and Palliative Care PDAF requires Anaesthesiology, and its Palliative Oncology PDCC requires Radiotherapy; similar course titles are not feeder eligibility.
- CMC's MBBS-level palliative certificate is up-skilling and explicitly does not qualify someone as a palliative specialist; it is not an additional specialist degree after MD.

Sources: [NBEMS DNB Palliative Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Palliative Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 8); [AIIMS July 2025 Fellowship in Pain Medicine requirements](https://docs.aiimsexams.ac.in/sites/prospectus-FP-July%202025-v3.pdf) (PDF page(s) 7)

**Open evidence gaps**

- A current 2026 advanced palliative-specific fellowship intake directly admitting MD Palliative Medicine was not verified beyond the older AIIMS example.
- No representative post-MD vacancy, salary or local saturation dataset was established.
- The cited institutional route does not verify DNB Palliative Medicine equivalence at AIIMS.

### Editorial career planning

Shortlist this for sustained patient and family care, symptom relief and communication, with a realistic map of institutions that fund dedicated posts. I would not treat social need as proof of a large private salary market.

- **After MD:** Dedicated palliative service or supported hospital role; check travel, home visits and on-call arrangements.
- **Further training:** Check conditional pain and other institutional programmes individually. The universal Medical Genetics feeder is a separate career pivot, not a palliative continuation.
- **Service leadership:** Compare an employed clinical/academic route with programme leadership, teaching and community-service development.

- A funded oncology, hospice, hospital or home-care role with a clear service mandate.
- Clinical communication, multidisciplinary leadership and symptom-control expertise.
- A pain fellowship may require additional post-PG experience; it does not establish universal procedural privileges.
- Who funds the post and how secure is the contract?
- Is there a dedicated team, medicine access and support for emotionally difficult care?
- Reliable private/corporate pay by city and stage remains a research gap.

## Pharmacology

MD/DNB Pharmacology supports several distinct careers: academic pharmacology, drug safety, clinical research and pharmaceutical/regulatory scientific work. DM Clinical Pharmacology is one verified NEET-SS route, and Medical Genetics is also currently permitted; neither is mandatory for every industry or teaching job.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **Senior residency / academic pharmacologist** — Medical college or teaching/research department. Teach pharmacology and therapeutics, assess students, conduct research and contribute to rational prescribing education. Progression to faculty depends on current appointment rules and vacancies. Sources: [MD Pharmacology revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-in-Pharmacology-(revised).pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 2)
- **Pharmacovigilance / drug-safety physician** — Hospital safety unit, pharmacovigilance centre, industry or research programme. Evaluate adverse-event information, causality and safety reporting; exact duties and entry requirements vary. CMC provides an institutional example of ward/ICU monitoring and serious-adverse-event review. Sources: [MD Pharmacology revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-in-Pharmacology-(revised).pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 2); [CMC Pharmacology department: services and training](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/pharmacology/) (Training: Fellowship on Applied Pharmacokinetics; Service)
- **Clinical research / drug-development role** — Research institute, trial unit or pharmaceutical industry. Apply trial methodology, drug-development knowledge, ethical/regulatory review and interpretation of evidence. Specific investigator, medical-monitor or company positions need their own eligibility and experience checks. Sources: [MD Pharmacology revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-in-Pharmacology-(revised).pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 2)
- **Regulatory / scientific writing / science-management work** — Regulatory body, research organization or pharmaceutical employer. The NMC curriculum explicitly identifies regulatory bodies, industry, scientific writing and science management as possible settings. It does not establish a current vacancy, role title, salary or selection advantage for every MD graduate. Sources: [MD Pharmacology revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-in-Pharmacology-(revised).pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 2)
- **Therapeutic drug monitoring and clinical pharmacology service** — Hospital clinical-pharmacology laboratory and clinical team. Drug-level interpretation, dose individualization and medication-use review are skill-based service options; access depends on the institution and local scope. Applied pharmacokinetics can deepen this rather than automatically create a stand-alone clinical practice. Sources: [CMC Pharmacology department: services and training](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/pharmacology/) (Training: Fellowship on Applied Pharmacokinetics; Service); [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / pharmacology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.35-36,60)

Any recognised MD/MS/DNB broad specialty is eligible; Medical Group question paper is required. This does not open the other Medical Group destinations. |

Any recognised MD/MS/DNB broad specialty is eligible; Medical Group question paper is required. This does not open the other Medical Group destinations. |

### Fellowships and focused training

FNB and institutional awards are separate routes; verify current registration, recognition, privileges, intake, fees and stipend. This is an evidenced selection, not an exhaustive national fellowship register.

- **Applied Pharmacokinetics — Christian Medical College Vellore**. Medical postdoctoral fellowship. Entry: Institution-specific medical postdoctoral fellowship selection; read the current course admission bulletin, not NEET-SS.. Eligibility: MD Pharmacology is the qualification printed in the 2026 prospectus; confirm DNB acceptance rather than assuming equivalence in this institutional route.. Duration: 1 year in 2026-2027 prospectus table; undated department page says 2 years. Obtain current written confirmation.. Prospectus is common to courses throughout the year and must be read with the course-specific admission bulletin. All listed fellowships are skills-development programmes, not equivalent to or replacements for NMC-recognized courses (p.36). Course tables do not establish a live application window or guaranteed seat; verify the current selection notice. Duration conflict: the dated prospectus lists one year, but the department page lists two; do not budget time/cost without registrar confirmation. Sources: [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / pharmacology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.35-36,60); [CMC Pharmacology department: services and training](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/pharmacology/) (Training: Fellowship on Applied Pharmacokinetics; Service)
- **Clinical Genetics — Christian Medical College Vellore**. Medical postdoctoral fellowship. Entry: Institution-specific medical postdoctoral fellowship selection; read the current course admission bulletin, not NEET-SS.. Eligibility: MD/DNB in any subject (or MS/DNB Obstetrics and Gynaecology), plus all course-specific admission conditions.. Duration: 2 years. Prospectus is common to courses throughout the year and must be read with the course-specific admission bulletin. All listed fellowships are skills-development programmes, not equivalent to or replacements for NMC-recognized courses (p.36). Course tables do not establish a live application window or guaranteed seat; verify the current selection notice. Sources: [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / genetics](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.33,36,46-47)

### Other pathways

- **PhD / translational research**. CMC lists Pharmacology among its PhD programmes. Consider a PhD only if the proposed methods, supervisor and funding fit a research career; current admission eligibility is separate from holding an MD. Sources: [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / phd](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF p.125)
- **Focused trial, safety or methods portfolio**. NMC competencies include clinical-trial processes, pharmacovigilance, health economics, regulatory/ethical principles, scientific communication and research. Relevant supervised work and a demonstrable methods portfolio can be evaluated alongside formal qualifications; no short certificate is presented as a substitute for a recognized specialty. Sources: [MD Pharmacology revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-in-Pharmacology-(revised).pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 2)
- **Patient-safety systems route**. SGPGI lists an institutional PDCC in Patient Safety including Infection Control with any broad specialty among its feeders. This is a systems/quality option, not a DM in clinical medicine. Sources: [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF brochure / safety](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp.4,6,12-16)

### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

Use separate job markets for academia, drug safety, clinical trials, regulatory work and pharmacokinetic services. The NMC curriculum describes a broadening field, but its 2022 demand language is not a current vacancy census or assurance of easy hiring.

**Evidence limits:** No current, geography-matched workforce supply/demand study or systematic vacancy census was established in this research. Curricula and advertised training programmes show scope and possible differentiation; they cannot measure market saturation, predict income, or guarantee employment.

- Read recent job descriptions for target roles: do they require MD, prior industry experience, trial experience or additional quantitative skills?
- Check whether your college offers genuine pharmacovigilance, clinical-trial and industry exposure rather than classroom discussion only.
- Ask alumni about entry-level duties, mentoring, contract status, location and time to their first relevant job.
- For TDM, check supervised assay interpretation, LC-MS/MS/HPLC access and actual clinician collaboration.
- Choose an intended setting first: an industry safety role, academic pharmacology and hospital dose-individualization service require different portfolios.
- Do not assume DM is necessary for every pharmaceutical role or guarantees a more senior corporate position.
- Resolve the CMC fellowship duration conflict before using it in a time or fee comparison.

Sources: [MD Pharmacology revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-in-Pharmacology-(revised).pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 2); [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / pharmacology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.35-36,60)

**Open evidence gaps**

- No complete current inventory of industry or regulatory vacancies was assembled in this route file.
- No direct FNB course specifically for Pharmacology was established in the FET 2025 Indian candidate list.
- INI-SS Clinical Pharmacology programmes and their institution/session-specific feeders remain to be audited separately.

### Editorial career planning

A strong candidate if you prefer drug science, evidence assessment and teaching to a conventional consultation practice. Choose between academia, clinical research and industry deliberately; one salary ladder cannot represent all three.

- **After MD:** Academic SR/teaching, trial or safety roles with qualification-specific recruitment.
- **Focused training / SS:** Compare Clinical Pharmacology and Medical Genetics routes with trial and pharmacokinetic training; check the admitting prospectus.
- **Established career:** Academic promotion or scientific/industry leadership; compare remuneration and benefits by role, not department name alone.

- A verified academic track with current teacher eligibility and research development.
- Demonstrable trial design, safety assessment, medical writing or medical-affairs capability for an actual industry role.
- Quantitative and regulatory skills that the employer explicitly values, rather than collecting certificates.
- Exclude PharmD/MPharm roles when benchmarking MD-specific pay unless the employer explicitly recruits both.
- Ask recent MD alumni about actual industry transitions, responsibilities and offers.

## Pathology

MD/DNB Pathology can lead directly toward diagnostic laboratory, hospital and academic work, with further training chosen around the actual reporting responsibility you want. Clinical haematology, oncopathology and medical genetics are separate verified NEET-SS routes; a short laboratory fellowship is not the same clinical qualification.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **Diagnostic pathologist** — Hospital department or diagnostic laboratory. Work in histopathology, cytology and haematology, integrating specimens, clinical information and ancillary tests into diagnostic reports. The breadth of an individual job depends on training, registration and privileges. Sources: [MD Pathology revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Pathology_(revised).pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 3)
- **Laboratory quality / service lead** — Hospital laboratory, laboratory network or independently organized service. Quality control, appropriate test selection, reporting standards, records and laboratory safety are explicit competencies. Ownership also requires business, infrastructure, staffing and applicable laboratory permissions; an MD does not itself fund or authorize every laboratory service. Sources: [MD Pathology revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Pathology_(revised).pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 3)
- **Senior residency and medical teaching pathway** — Teaching hospital or medical college. Build supervised diagnostic responsibility, teach medical/allied-health learners and develop an academic portfolio. A faculty appointment requires the current recruitment and faculty-eligibility rules in addition to the degree. Sources: [MD Pathology revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Pathology_(revised).pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 3)
- **Diagnostic / translational researcher** — Academic department, cancer centre or research team. Plan and carry out pathology research; stronger molecular, cytogenetic or organ-system expertise can support a focused research or referral role. Fellowship access must match its named feeder. Sources: [MD Pathology revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Pathology_(revised).pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 3); [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / pathology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.33-36,46,51-53)

### Verified NEET-SS destination seats

| Award | Specialty | Seats | Entry / feeder notes |
|---|---|---:|---|
| DM | Clinical Haematology | 39 | NEET-SS: Medical Group; MCC SS counselling; Eligibility and seat availability are different; this is the dated initial matrix, not an admission guarantee. |
| DrNB | Clinical Haematology | 50 | NEET-SS: Medical Group; MCC SS counselling; Eligibility and seat availability are different; this is the dated initial matrix, not an admission guarantee. |

### Fellowships and focused training

FNB and institutional awards are separate routes; verify current registration, recognition, privileges, intake, fees and stipend. This is an evidenced selection, not an exhaustive national fellowship register.

- **Molecular Haematology — Christian Medical College Vellore**. Medical postdoctoral fellowship. Entry: Institution-specific medical postdoctoral fellowship selection; read the current course admission bulletin, not NEET-SS.. Eligibility: MD/DNB Pathology.. Duration: 2 years. Prospectus is common to courses throughout the year and must be read with the course-specific admission bulletin. All listed fellowships are skills-development programmes, not equivalent to or replacements for NMC-recognized courses (p.36). Course tables do not establish a live application window or guaranteed seat; verify the current selection notice. ### Other pathways

- **PhD / research development**. CMC lists PhD programmes in several biomedical/clinical disciplines. A PhD may support a research-focused career, but eligibility, supervisor availability and funded admission are separate checks; it is not required for every diagnostic job. Sources: [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / phd](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF p.125)
- **Patient safety and infection-control administration**. SGPGI 2026 lists a one-year PDCC in Patient Safety including Infection Control accepting Hospital Administration, Microbiology or any broad specialty. It is an optional institutional systems route rather than an automatic change of clinical specialty. Sources: [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF brochure / safety](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp.4,6,12-16)

### Salary and stipend observations

- **Assistant Professor (regular/deputation pay scale), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 101,500–167,400 per month; Pay-matrix basic. Stage: Postgraduate academic career: Assistant Professor. Qualification: Recognised MD/MS or accepted equivalent in the concerned broad specialty; relevant DM/MCh/equivalent additionally required for superspecialty posts.. Experience: Three years of recognised teaching/research after the qualifying MD/MS/equivalent for broad specialties; the ad has separate, qualification-dependent rules for superspecialties.. Level12 monthly BASIC scale; applicable allowances and NPA are separate and have not been calculated. Scale maximum is a progression endpoint, not an advertised fresh-graduate package or guaranteed offer. Mapped specialties have a nonzero vacancy at this rank in the dated advertisement; category, selection and full eligibility still apply. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli faculty recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/FACULTY/2026/467](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C118) (pp2-6 qualifications/experience; p7 pay tables; pp8-9 department/rank vacancies; p15 contractual conditions)
- **Assistant Professor (contractual appointment), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 142,506–142,506 per month; Consolidated remuneration. Stage: Postgraduate academic career: Assistant Professor. Qualification: Concerned postgraduate specialty and full faculty eligibility required.. Experience: Three years of recognised teaching/research after the qualifying MD/MS/equivalent for broad specialties; the ad has separate, qualification-dependent rules for superspecialties.. Advertised monthly lump-sum contractual remuneration; do not add regular-pay allowances by assumption. Initial contract is one year or until alternative arrangements, whichever earlier; other conditions are in Annexure 2. Retired faculty consultant designations/accommodation deductions have separate conditions; these are not used as a general private-consultant salary. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli faculty recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/FACULTY/2026/467](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C118) (pp2-6 qualifications/experience; p7 pay tables; pp8-9 department/rank vacancies; p15 contractual conditions)
- **Additional Professor (regular/deputation pay scale), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 148,200–211,400 per month; Pay-matrix basic. Stage: Postgraduate academic career: Additional Professor. Qualification: Recognised MD/MS or accepted equivalent in the concerned broad specialty; relevant DM/MCh/equivalent additionally required for superspecialty posts.. Experience: Ten years of recognised teaching/research after qualifying MD/MS/equivalent for broad specialties; seven years after a recognised three-year DM/MCh for superspecialty appointments, with other routes separately specified.. Level13-A2+ monthly BASIC scale; applicable allowances and NPA are separate and have not been calculated. Scale maximum is a progression endpoint, not an advertised fresh-graduate package or guaranteed offer. Mapped specialties have a nonzero vacancy at this rank in the dated advertisement; category, selection and full eligibility still apply. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli faculty recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/FACULTY/2026/467](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C118) (pp2-6 qualifications/experience; p7 pay tables; pp8-9 department/rank vacancies; p15 contractual conditions)
- **Additional Professor (contractual appointment), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 200,000–200,000 per month; Consolidated remuneration. Stage: Postgraduate academic career: Additional Professor. Qualification: Concerned postgraduate specialty and full faculty eligibility required.. Experience: Ten years of recognised teaching/research after qualifying MD/MS/equivalent for broad specialties; seven years after a recognised three-year DM/MCh for superspecialty appointments, with other routes separately specified.. Advertised monthly lump-sum contractual remuneration; do not add regular-pay allowances by assumption. Initial contract is one year or until alternative arrangements, whichever earlier; other conditions are in Annexure 2. Retired faculty consultant designations/accommodation deductions have separate conditions; these are not used as a general private-consultant salary. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli faculty recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/FACULTY/2026/467](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C118) (pp2-6 qualifications/experience; p7 pay tables; pp8-9 department/rank vacancies; p15 contractual conditions)
- **Senior Resident (ad-hoc), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 67,700–67,700 per month; Pay-matrix basic. Stage: Post-MD/MS/DNB senior residency. Qualification: MD/MS/DNB in the concerned broad specialty or equivalent; registration and full ad conditions.. Experience: Postgraduate qualification is required; no universal additional experience period is stated in the cited broad-specialty criteria.. Advertised minimum monthly Level 11 basic plus NPA for medical personnel; gross and take-home are not calculated. Initial ad-hoc tenure six months, potentially extendable; regular-service benefit exclusions apply. ENT has zero vacancies in this particular advertisement and is intentionally not mapped; Nuclear Medicine, Family, Geriatric and Palliative Medicine are not advertised here. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli ad-hoc Senior Resident recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/SR/2026/532](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C124) (pp1-2 departments and vacancies; p3 qualification, pay and appointment conditions; p4 excluded regular-service benefits)
- **Senior Resident (Hospital Services), SGPGIMS Lucknow, Lucknow, Uttar Pradesh (Tier 2)**: INR 67,700–67,700 per month; Pay-matrix basic. Stage: Post-MD/MS/DNB senior residency. Experience: Postgraduate entry criteria are department-specific; any superspecialty or other additional requirement must be checked in the brochure.. Entry monthly Level 11 basic INR 67700 plus applicable allowances. Full pay-scale upper endpoint INR 208700 is not presented as an entry offer. Sponsored trainees are not paid salary/stipend by SGPGIMS; their sponsoring authority is responsible. Sources: [SGPGIMS 2026 PDAF/PDCC/SR(Hospital Services) information brochure, I/55/ER/ACAD/2025-26](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (pp7-10 course-specific SR qualifications; p12 sponsored-candidate conditions; p16 salary/emoluments)
- **DM/MCh trainee, year 1, Tata Memorial Centre / Tata Memorial Hospital, Mumbai, Maharashtra (Tier 1)**: INR 133,623–133,623 per month; Training stipend. Stage: Super-specialty training year 1. Qualification: Admission to an eligible TMC DM/MCh course through NEET-SS and MCC counselling; parent eligibility is destination-specific.. Experience: Completed eligible postgraduate feeder qualification; this payment is during further training.. 2025 admission guidelines monthly stipend, corroborated by the signed 7 September 2026 FY2026-27 disclosure at the same amounts; not post-training consultant income or evidence of a stipend increase. Parent mapping is limited to actual TMC DM/MCh destinations in the initial matrix and eligible feeders; it does not imply every course is open to every mapped parent. Annual fees, hostel INR 9000/month subject to revision, deposits and a one-year service bond are separate; no net-pay subtraction is calculated. ### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

Assess general diagnostic employment separately from a referral niche, academic recruitment and the economics of owning a laboratory; the sources do not justify calling pathology universally saturated or undersupplied.

**Evidence limits:** No current, geography-matched workforce supply/demand study or systematic vacancy census was established in this research. Curricula and advertised training programmes show scope and possible differentiation; they cannot measure market saturation, predict income, or guarantee employment.

- Collect actual vacancies, contract terms and reporting responsibilities in your target city and nearby districts.
- Ask who gets independent sign-out, second opinions, tumour-board participation and hands-on IHC/flow/molecular exposure.
- For ownership, model sample sources, test mix, quality systems, equipment/staff costs and dependence on referral arrangements.
- Check whether the proposed fellowship changes your day-to-day reporting competence or only adds a certificate.
- Choose programmes with supervised independent reporting and exposure to both common material and difficult referrals.
- If your intended work is clinical patient management of blood disorders, examine the DM/DrNB Clinical Haematology route rather than equating it with laboratory haematopathology.
- Do not infer that all blood-bank, molecular or organ-pathology roles require the same additional award.

Sources: [MD Pathology revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Pathology_(revised).pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 3); [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / pathology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.33-36,46,51-53)

**Open evidence gaps**

- The separate INI-SS versus NEET-SS comparison audits January 2027 institute-specific Pathology feeder rules. INI session seat counts are not audited here; use the comparison and intended-session seat notices separately.
- TMC catalogue options need their current specialty-specific advertisement; no live intake is inferred.
- No national saturation measure or automatic income premium for a fellowship was established.

### Editorial career planning

Shortlist if you enjoy diagnostic reasoning from samples and sustained detail. I would favour excellent morphology, reporting, clinicopathological communication and quality systems before choosing a high-value niche.

- **After MD:** Supervised reporting, hospital/lab employment or academic SR; check case mix and sign-out responsibilities.
- **Focused training / SS:** Separate recognised DM/DrNB routes from institutional diagnostic fellowships and confirm feeders.
- **Established career:** Subspecialist reporting, academic progression or lab/cluster leadership. A senior cluster role is not a fresh-MD comparator.

- Diagnostic breadth and reliable turnaround with appropriate subspecialist backup.
- Focused competence in areas such as haematology, oncopathology or molecular diagnostics, matched to the laboratory's work.
- Laboratory ownership and leadership introduce collection, staffing, accreditation and capital responsibilities.
- Who provides second opinions and authorises complex reports?
- What is the actual sample volume per pathologist and how are urgent cases covered?
- Do not infer job losses or inevitable salary decline from automation discussions alone.

## Geriatric Medicine

MD/DNB Geriatric Medicine is a specialist route for older adults across acute illness, multimorbidity, function, frailty and transitions of care. It is not automatically the feeder equivalent of MD General Medicine for every DM, while targeted institutional options can explicitly accept it.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **Geriatrician / older-adult consultant** — Hospital inpatient/OPD, day hospital, home or community service. The curriculum targets comprehensive geriatric assessment, acute and chronic illness, cognition, gait, nutrition, disability and individualized care plans. Practice remains subject to registration, competence and facility arrangements. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Geriatric Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Geriatric Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Rehabilitation and transition-of-care clinician** — Multidisciplinary hospital, intermediate-care or community team. Assess frailty and function, coordinate rehabilitation, plan discharge and transfer between hospital and home; this is distinct from automatic qualification as a neurologist or psychiatrist. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Geriatric Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Geriatric Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Teaching / geriatrics and gerontology research** — Academic department or older-person service. The curriculum includes undergraduate/postgraduate teaching, research and older-person health programmes. Actual faculty recruitment and public-service eligibility depend on current rules and vacancies. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Geriatric Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Geriatric Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)

Any recognised MD/MS/DNB broad specialty is eligible; Medical Group question paper is required. This does not open the other Medical Group destinations. |
| DrNB | Medical Genetics | 4 | NEET-SS: Medical Group; MCC SS counselling; Eligibility and seat availability are different; this is the dated initial matrix, not an admission guarantee. Any recognised MD/MS/DNB broad specialty is eligible; Medical Group question paper is required. This does not open the other Medical Group destinations. |

### Superspecialty route details

- **DM / DrNB Medical Genetics** (Verified feeder). NEET-SS 2025 and applicable counselling. MD/DNB Geriatric Medicine is covered by Any MD/MS/DNB Broad Specialty. This does not establish the organ-specialty feeder eligibility of MD General Medicine. Duration: DrNB: three years after MD/MS/DNB under the official programme overview. For DM, confirm the current admitting-institute prospectus.. Sources: [NEET-SS 2025 Medical Genetics any-broad-specialty feeder](https://nbe.edu.in/IB/NEET-SS%202025%20information%20bulletin.pdf) (PDF page(s) 116, 118); [NBEMS DrNB Super Specialty programme duration and entry routes](https://natboard.edu.in/dnbsuper) (DrNB overview / Admission and Registration)

### Fellowships and focused training

FNB and institutional awards are separate routes; verify current registration, recognition, privileges, intake, fees and stipend. This is an evidenced selection, not an exhaustive national fellowship register.

- **Diabetology — CMC Vellore**. Post-doctoral institutional fellowship. Entry: Institutional selection; read current admission bulletin with the CMC 2026-2027 prospectus. Eligibility: MD/DNB General Medicine / Geriatric explicitly listed. Duration: 1 year. Institutional skills development, not equivalent to or replacement for an NMC-recognized MD/DM/MCh/DrNB. Verify current assessment, recognition and clinical privileges. Sources: [CMC 2026-2027 physician, family and geriatric fellowship feeders](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 33, 34, 35); [CMC fellowship recognition and full-time training restrictions](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 36)
- **Clinical Genetics — CMC Vellore**. Post-doctoral institutional fellowship. Entry: Institutional selection; read current admission bulletin with the CMC 2026-2027 prospectus. Eligibility: MD/DNB in any subject; confirm exact named qualification with admissions. Duration: 2 years. Institutional skills development, not equivalent to or replacement for an NMC-recognized MD/DM/MCh/DrNB. Verify current assessment, recognition and clinical privileges. Sources: [CMC 2026-2027 physician, family and geriatric fellowship feeders](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 33, 34, 35); [CMC fellowship recognition and full-time training restrictions](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 36)
- **Patient Safety including Infection Control — SGPGIMS Lucknow**. PDCC. Entry: SGPGI 2026 institutional entrance/selection. Eligibility: MD/DNB Hospital Administration / Microbiology / Any Broad Specialty. The printed qualification row says MD/DNB; an MS-only holder should obtain written eligibility confirmation rather than assume equivalence.. Duration: 1 year under the PDCC duration rule. Institutional certificate focused on patient safety; not an organ-specialty DM/MCh/DrNB. Obtain written confirmation of the exact feeder, current recognition and clinical or academic portability. Sources: [SGPGI 2026 Patient Safety including Infection Control eligibility](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF page 6, programme and essential-qualification table); [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF admission and SR eligibility brochure](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF page(s) 5, 6, 7, 16)

### Other pathways

- **Focused older-person services**. Memory/falls, medication review, rehabilitation and discharge coordination can be developed within the geriatric scope with appropriate multidisciplinary colleagues. They do not confer another organ-specialty registration or prove local paying demand. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Geriatric Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Geriatric Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Geriatric Mental Health is a different feeder route**. The NEET-SS 2025 table lists Psychiatry as the feeder for DM Geriatric Mental Health. Treating older patients or holding MD Geriatric Medicine does not establish that eligibility. Sources: [NEET-SS 2025 Paediatrics feeder table and award clarification](https://nbe.edu.in/IB/NEET-SS%202025%20information%20bulletin.pdf) (PDF page(s) 115, 118)

### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

A universal saturated/not-saturated label is not established for geriatric medicine. Local paid demand and service design need separate assessment.

**Evidence limits:** Official training documents establish roles and admissions, not a specialty-by-city workforce survey, secure vacancies or earnings. Growing clinical need does not by itself create funded posts or reimbursed care.

- Identify named geriatrician posts separately from general medical-officer or internal-medicine posts; confirm that MD Geriatrics is accepted in the written advertisement.
- Investigate hospital referrals, home/day-care funding, rehabilitation and nursing support for frail patients.
- Check consultation length, travel and caregiver coordination costs against the actual service contract or payer model.
- Before an extra course, ask whether it adds supervised skills beyond the comprehensive geriatrics already taught in MD/DNB.
- Medical Genetics is verified through the universal broad-specialty feeder; Cardiology/Neurology/Endocrinology DM eligibility is not inferred from a geriatrics title.
- CMC's one-year Geriatric Medicine fellowship is listed for General Medicine/Family Medicine graduates; it is not an additional higher geriatrics degree automatically intended for MD Geriatrics.
- Do not confuse DM Geriatric Mental Health with MD Geriatric Medicine: its NEET-SS feeder is Psychiatry, rather than all clinicians treating older adults.
- A low count of geriatric departments is not enough to conclude either scarcity of jobs or guaranteed opportunity.

Sources: [NBEMS DNB Geriatric Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Geriatric Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5); [CMC 2026-2027 physician, family and geriatric fellowship feeders](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 33, 34, 35)

**Open evidence gaps**

- No representative geriatrician vacancy, local market-density or post-MD income dataset was established.
- Additional course equivalence for specific academic posts must be checked against current recruitment rules.

### Editorial career planning

A good fit for someone who values multimorbidity, function and continuity in older adults. I would investigate funded roles and the local recognition of geriatric services before assuming a demographic trend translates into specialist employment.

- **After MD:** Geriatric clinical service, teaching or supported continuity practice; check available funded posts.
- **Focused training:** Use dated eligibility for programmes such as Diabetology; do not assume all General Medicine SS feeders apply.
- **Established service:** Older-person care leadership, home-care or integrated services, with realistic referral and payment arrangements.

- A clear older-person service with multidisciplinary and rehabilitation support.
- Hospital, academic, home-care or continuity-clinic employment with defined responsibilities.
- Competence and communication that distinguish the service from a generic adult-medicine clinic.
- Does the employer specifically recognise MD Geriatrics for the advertised post?
- What are the referral pathways and responsibilities for acute medical emergencies?
- Tier-specific salaries are not established from the limited adverts found.

## Family Medicine

MD/DNB Family Medicine supports comprehensive, continuous care across ages in primary and secondary settings. It is a distinct generalist specialty; its broad clinical work must not be used to assume all General Medicine superspecialty feeders are available.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **Family physician / continuity-care generalist** — Community clinic, primary/secondary hospital, public or private practice. Provide whole-person, lifespan and family-centred care, manage common acute/chronic problems and coordinate appropriate referral. The curriculum covers broad settings rather than guaranteeing every employer will accept the qualification for a differently named post. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Family Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for NBEMS DNB Family Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 12)
- **Rural / secondary-care generalist** — Community Health Centre or appropriately equipped generalist service. Develop supervised emergency, child, maternity and medical/surgical skills matched to local needs. Curriculum aspirations are not automatic privileges for every operation, anesthesia technique or obstetric service. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Family Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for NBEMS DNB Family Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 12)
- **Clinical quality, education and service development** — Primary-care team, academic or community programme. The curriculum includes clinical audit, research, quality/accreditation, communication and multidisciplinary collaboration. Faculty recruitment remains governed by current qualification and appointment rules. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Family Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for NBEMS DNB Family Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 12)

### Fellowships and focused training

FNB and institutional awards are separate routes; verify current registration, recognition, privileges, intake, fees and stipend. This is an evidenced selection, not an exhaustive national fellowship register.

- **Geriatric Medicine — CMC Vellore**. Post-doctoral institutional fellowship. Entry: Institutional selection; read current admission bulletin with the CMC 2026-2027 prospectus. Eligibility: MD/DNB General Medicine or Family Medicine explicitly listed. Duration: 1 year. Institutional skills development, not equivalent to or replacement for an NMC-recognized MD/DM/MCh/DrNB. Verify current assessment, recognition and clinical privileges. Sources: [CMC 2026-2027 physician, family and geriatric fellowship feeders](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 33, 34, 35); [CMC fellowship recognition and full-time training restrictions](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 36)
- **Obstetric Medicine — CMC Vellore**. Post-doctoral institutional fellowship. Entry: Institutional selection; read current admission bulletin with the CMC 2026-2027 prospectus. Eligibility: MD/DNB General Medicine or Family Medicine explicitly listed. Duration: 1 year. Institutional skills development, not equivalent to or replacement for an NMC-recognized MD/DM/MCh/DrNB. Verify current assessment, recognition and clinical privileges. Sources: [CMC 2026-2027 physician, family and geriatric fellowship feeders](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 33, 34, 35); [CMC fellowship recognition and full-time training restrictions](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 36)
- **Clinical Genetics — CMC Vellore**. Post-doctoral institutional fellowship. Entry: Institutional selection; read current admission bulletin with the CMC 2026-2027 prospectus. Eligibility: MD/DNB in any subject; written admission confirmation advised. Duration: 2 years. Institutional skills development, not equivalent to or replacement for an NMC-recognized MD/DM/MCh/DrNB. Verify current assessment, recognition and clinical privileges. Sources: [CMC 2026-2027 physician, family and geriatric fellowship feeders](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 33, 34, 35); [CMC fellowship recognition and full-time training restrictions](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 36)
- **Distance Fellowship in Diabetes Management (DFID) — CMC Vellore**. Distance fellowship / blended up-skilling. Entry: No entrance examination per prospectus; follow the current distance-education application process. Eligibility: MD/DNB General/Internal/Family Medicine listed among eligible categories; medical-council registration required. Duration: 1 year. Institutional skills development, not equivalent to or replacement for an NMC-recognized MD/DM/MCh/DrNB. Verify current assessment, recognition and clinical privileges. Sources: [CMC Distance Fellowship in Diabetes Management eligibility](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pages 69 and 73, DFID course list and eligibility)
- **Patient Safety including Infection Control — SGPGIMS Lucknow**. PDCC. Entry: SGPGI 2026 institutional entrance/selection. Eligibility: MD/DNB Hospital Administration / Microbiology / Any Broad Specialty. The printed qualification row says MD/DNB; an MS-only holder should obtain written eligibility confirmation rather than assume equivalence.. Duration: 1 year under the PDCC duration rule. Institutional certificate focused on patient safety; not an organ-specialty DM/MCh/DrNB. Obtain written confirmation of the exact feeder, current recognition and clinical or academic portability. Sources: [SGPGI 2026 Patient Safety including Infection Control eligibility](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF page 6, programme and essential-qualification table); [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF admission and SR eligibility brochure](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF page(s) 5, 6, 7, 16)

### Other pathways

- **Continuity clinic and coordinated care**. A family practice can focus on continuity, prevention, chronic disease and appropriate referral across ages. It should retain its generalist identity rather than present a short course as an MD General Medicine or DM replacement. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Family Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for NBEMS DNB Family Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 12); [CMC Distance Fellowship in Diabetes Management eligibility](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pages 69 and 73, DFID course list and eligibility)

### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

A universal saturated/not-saturated label is not established for family medicine. Local paid demand and service design need separate assessment.

**Evidence limits:** Official training documents establish roles and admissions, not a specialty-by-city workforce survey, secure vacancies or earnings. Growing clinical need does not by itself create funded posts or reimbursed care.

- Check written job-ad acceptance of MD/DNB Family Medicine rather than assume interchangeability with MD Medicine or Community Medicine.
- Assess household continuity, local primary-care competition, access and willingness to pay for repeat follow-up.
- For a CHC/secondary-care role, verify staffing, emergency backup, referral links and the exact procedures expected.
- Check whether salaried employment permits outside clinic work, and model follow-up time and overheads before investing.
- Current verified NEET-SS eligibility here is Medical Genetics, not the whole General Medicine list.
- Named CMC routes demonstrate specific Family Medicine eligibility for geriatrics and obstetric medicine; that does not make every institutional fellowship available.
- An MBBS-level or distance PG Diploma in Family Medicine is not the same qualification as MD/DNB Family Medicine, even when course names overlap.
- Further focused up-skilling is optional and should preserve competence, referral and clear credential disclosure.

Sources: [NBEMS DNB Family Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for NBEMS DNB Family Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 12); [CMC 2026-2027 physician, family and geriatric fellowship feeders](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 33, 34, 35)

**Open evidence gaps**

- No representative city-wise funded vacancy, saturation or net practice income dataset was established.
- A current official national mapping of every employer title accepting Family Medicine was not established.

### Editorial career planning

Shortlist for broad, continuing care across life stages and comfort with uncertainty. I would select training that offers supervised clinical breadth and an employer market that explicitly accepts Family Medicine qualifications.

- **After MD/DNB:** Primary care or explicitly accepted hospital roles; verify the qualification in the advert.
- **Focused training:** Review FNB/institutional entry criteria individually. Family Medicine is not automatically interchangeable with General Medicine in SS eligibility.
- **Established career:** Longitudinal practice or primary-care leadership; model the patient base and clinic costs before assuming business profit.

- Continuity relationships, a defined primary-care population and safe referral arrangements.
- Hospital, community, corporate primary-care or independent-clinic roles within verified privileges.
- Communication, prevention and coordinated care, rather than a narrow procedure-based model.
- Search results titled family physician can actually require MD General Medicine; those are not equivalent offers.
- Check who provides paediatric, obstetric, surgical and emergency backup for a broad-scope post.

## General Medicine

MD/DNB General Medicine can support a general-physician career or serve as a feeder for numerous organ and system superspecialties. Broad practice, NEET-SS, FNB after a qualifying superspecialty and institutional focused training remain distinct choices; none automatically guarantees higher income.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **General physician / junior consultant** — General medicine OPD, inpatient hospital or community-linked practice. The official programme goal is to function as a junior consultant offering preventive, promotive, curative and rehabilitative care. Independent work remains subject to competence, registration, hospital privileges and appropriate specialist referral. Sources: [NBEMS DNB General Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB General Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Senior resident / hospital physician** — Teaching, service or multidisciplinary hospital. Consolidate acute-care reasoning, longitudinal adult care, safe procedures and multidisciplinary coordination; a job title does not automatically authorize every organ-specialty intervention. Sources: [NBEMS DNB General Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB General Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Teaching, clinical audit or research** — Academic department or clinical service. Curriculum includes case discussion, seminars, journal clubs and critical appraisal. Recruitment to a faculty rank requires the current teacher-eligibility and institution-specific rules. Sources: [NBEMS DNB General Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB General Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)

### Fellowships and focused training

FNB and institutional awards are separate routes; verify current registration, recognition, privileges, intake, fees and stipend. This is an evidenced selection, not an exhaustive national fellowship register.

- **Advanced Arthritis, Rheumatism and Autoimmunity — CMC Vellore**. Post-doctoral institutional fellowship. Entry: Institutional selection; read the CMC 2026-2027 prospectus with the current course-specific admission bulletin. Eligibility: MD/DNB General Medicine or Paediatrics. Duration: 2 years. Institutional skills development, not equivalent to or replacement for an NMC-recognized MD/DM/MCh/DrNB. Verify current assessment, recognition and clinical privileges. Sources: [CMC 2026-2027 physician, family and geriatric fellowship feeders](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 33, 34, 35); [CMC fellowship recognition and full-time training restrictions](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 36)

Eligibility: MD/DNB General Medicine or Geriatric. Duration: 1 year. Institutional skills development, not equivalent to or replacement for an NMC-recognized MD/DM/MCh/DrNB. Verify current assessment, recognition and clinical privileges.
