### Other pathways

- **Advanced FNB after a relevant DM/DrNB**. FET 2025 lists Interventional Cardiology and Cardiac Electrophysiology after DM/DrNB Cardiology, and Stroke Medicine after DM/DrNB Neurology. These are second-stage options after the relevant SS, not direct MD General Medicine entries. The old FNB catalogue must not be treated as a list of current offered General Medicine fellowships. Sources: [FET 2025 Indian-candidate feeder annexure](https://www.nbe.edu.in/IB/FET%202025%20information%20bulletin%2012.01.2026_v2.pdf) (PDF page(s) 130, 131); [FNB duration, recognition and teaching-faculty limitation](https://www.nbe.edu.in/IB/FET%202025%20information%20bulletin%2012.01.2026_v2.pdf) (PDF page(s) 122, 124)

### Salary and stipend observations

- **Assistant Professor (regular/deputation pay scale), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 101,500–167,400 per month; Pay-matrix basic. Stage: Postgraduate academic career: Assistant Professor. Qualification: Recognised MD/MS or accepted equivalent in the concerned broad specialty; relevant DM/MCh/equivalent additionally required for superspecialty posts.. Experience: Three years of recognised teaching/research after the qualifying MD/MS/equivalent for broad specialties; the ad has separate, qualification-dependent rules for superspecialties.. Level12 monthly BASIC scale; applicable allowances and NPA are separate and have not been calculated. Scale maximum is a progression endpoint, not an advertised fresh-graduate package or guaranteed offer. Mapped specialties have a nonzero vacancy at this rank in the dated advertisement; category, selection and full eligibility still apply. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli faculty recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/FACULTY/2026/467](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C118) (pp2-6 qualifications/experience; p7 pay tables; pp8-9 department/rank vacancies; p15 contractual conditions)
- **Assistant Professor (contractual appointment), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 142,506–142,506 per month; Consolidated remuneration. Stage: Postgraduate academic career: Assistant Professor. Qualification: Concerned postgraduate specialty and full faculty eligibility required.. Experience: Three years of recognised teaching/research after the qualifying MD/MS/equivalent for broad specialties; the ad has separate, qualification-dependent rules for superspecialties.. Advertised monthly lump-sum contractual remuneration; do not add regular-pay allowances by assumption. Initial contract is one year or until alternative arrangements, whichever earlier; other conditions are in Annexure 2. Retired faculty consultant designations/accommodation deductions have separate conditions; these are not used as a general private-consultant salary. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli faculty recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/FACULTY/2026/467](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C118) (pp2-6 qualifications/experience; p7 pay tables; pp8-9 department/rank vacancies; p15 contractual conditions)
- **Associate Professor (regular/deputation pay scale), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 138,300–209,200 per month; Pay-matrix basic. Stage: Postgraduate academic career: Associate Professor. Qualification: Recognised MD/MS or accepted equivalent in the concerned broad specialty; relevant DM/MCh/equivalent additionally required for superspecialty posts.. Experience: Six years of recognised teaching/research after the qualifying MD/MS/equivalent for broad specialties; three years after a recognised three-year DM/MCh for superspecialty appointments, with other historical routes separately specified.. Level13-A1+ monthly BASIC scale; applicable allowances and NPA are separate and have not been calculated. Scale maximum is a progression endpoint, not an advertised fresh-graduate package or guaranteed offer. Mapped specialties have a nonzero vacancy at this rank in the dated advertisement; category, selection and full eligibility still apply. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli faculty recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/FACULTY/2026/467](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C118) (pp2-6 qualifications/experience; p7 pay tables; pp8-9 department/rank vacancies; p15 contractual conditions)
- **Associate Professor (contractual appointment), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 188,000–188,000 per month; Consolidated remuneration. Stage: Postgraduate academic career: Associate Professor. Qualification: Concerned postgraduate specialty and full faculty eligibility required.. Experience: Six years of recognised teaching/research after the qualifying MD/MS/equivalent for broad specialties; three years after a recognised three-year DM/MCh for superspecialty appointments, with other historical routes separately specified.. Advertised monthly lump-sum contractual remuneration; do not add regular-pay allowances by assumption. Initial contract is one year or until alternative arrangements, whichever earlier; other conditions are in Annexure 2. Retired faculty consultant designations/accommodation deductions have separate conditions; these are not used as a general private-consultant salary. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli faculty recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/FACULTY/2026/467](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C118) (pp2-6 qualifications/experience; p7 pay tables; pp8-9 department/rank vacancies; p15 contractual conditions)
- **Senior Resident (ad-hoc), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 67,700–67,700 per month; Pay-matrix basic. Stage: Post-MD/MS/DNB senior residency. Qualification: MD/MS/DNB in the concerned broad specialty or equivalent; registration and full ad conditions.. Experience: Postgraduate qualification is required; no universal additional experience period is stated in the cited broad-specialty criteria.. Advertised minimum monthly Level 11 basic plus NPA for medical personnel; gross and take-home are not calculated. Initial ad-hoc tenure six months, potentially extendable; regular-service benefit exclusions apply. ENT has zero vacancies in this particular advertisement and is intentionally not mapped; Nuclear Medicine, Family, Geriatric and Palliative Medicine are not advertised here. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli ad-hoc Senior Resident recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/SR/2026/532](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C124) (pp1-2 departments and vacancies; p3 qualification, pay and appointment conditions; p4 excluded regular-service benefits)
- **Senior Resident (Hospital Services), SGPGIMS Lucknow, Lucknow, Uttar Pradesh (Tier 2)**: INR 67,700–67,700 per month; Pay-matrix basic. Stage: Post-MD/MS/DNB senior residency. Qualification: Course-specific recognised postgraduate qualification: medicine/paediatrics clinical departments; pathology/lab-medicine routes; Nuclear Medicine; Radiodiagnosis; MD/DNB Radiotherapy for Radiotherapy SRHS; MS/DNB General Surgery for named surgical SRHS departments, as listed in pp7-10.. Experience: Postgraduate entry criteria are department-specific; any superspecialty or other additional requirement must be checked in the brochure.. Entry monthly Level 11 basic INR 67700 plus applicable allowances. Full pay-scale upper endpoint INR 208700 is not presented as an entry offer. Sponsored trainees are not paid salary/stipend by SGPGIMS; their sponsoring authority is responsible. Sources: [SGPGIMS 2026 PDAF/PDCC/SR(Hospital Services) information brochure, I/55/ER/ACAD/2025-26](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (pp7-10 course-specific SR qualifications; p12 sponsored-candidate conditions; p16 salary/emoluments)
- **DM/MCh trainee, year 1, Tata Memorial Centre / Tata Memorial Hospital, Mumbai, Maharashtra (Tier 1)**: INR 133,623–133,623 per month; Training stipend. Stage: Super-specialty training year 1. Qualification: Admission to an eligible TMC DM/MCh course through NEET-SS and MCC counselling; parent eligibility is destination-specific.. Experience: Completed eligible postgraduate feeder qualification; this payment is during further training.. 2025 admission guidelines monthly stipend, corroborated by the signed 7 September 2026 FY2026-27 disclosure at the same amounts; not post-training consultant income or evidence of a stipend increase. The website link label says FY2025-26; the scanned linked document explicitly says FY2026-2027. Its date/heading govern this source description. Parent mapping is limited to actual TMC DM/MCh destinations in the initial matrix and eligible feeders; it does not imply every course is open to every mapped parent. Annual fees, hostel INR 9000/month subject to revision, deposits and a one-year service bond are separate; no net-pay subtraction is calculated. ### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

A universal saturated/not-saturated label is not established for general medicine. Evaluate the location, funded role, skill mix and service model.

**Evidence limits:** Curricula and admissions data are not measured local job demand, hiring competition or take-home income. Added training seats and patient need do not establish funded vacancies or a profitable independent service.

- Separate salaried general-physician/SR vacancies from consultant jobs that explicitly require a DM or a patient/referral base.
- For the intended town, compare adult OPD/inpatient demand, referral relationships, payer mix and the current supply of physicians.
- Ask whether a hospital supplies ICU support and appropriate specialist cover, and inspect the actual on-call rota.
- Compare the opportunity cost of further training with verified learning, written job requirements and the work you want; avoid a degree-only income calculation.
- The verified NEET-SS 2025 list has thirteen destination specialties; each has its own award, seats and curriculum. Those eligibility routes do not prove that every award has a seat in the current round.
- A general-physician career remains a clinical endpoint; specialization should follow desired work and structured competence rather than peer pressure.
- An institutional Diabetology or Pulmonology fellowship is not the same award as DM Endocrinology or DM Pulmonary Medicine.
- Current matrix capacity, local funded vacancies and salary are separate evidence layers.

Sources: [NBEMS DNB General Medicine curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB General Medicine&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5); [NEET-SS 2025 General Medicine feeder table](https://nbe.edu.in/IB/NEET-SS%202025%20information%20bulletin.pdf) (PDF page(s) 114)

**Open evidence gaps**

- DM duration is not independently established by the DrNB overview; check the current institute prospectus.
- No representative city-wise physician saturation or long-term income trajectory was established.
- A fellowship's portability to academic posts or another country requires separate current recognition checks.

### Editorial career planning

One of the clearest options for preserving multiple later career choices, if you genuinely like adult medicine and acute care. The number of SS destinations is an option count, not an instruction that everyone must superspecialise.

- **After MD:** SR, junior consultant, physician or academic roles; compare emergency cover and responsibility.
- **DM/DrNB training:** Compare eligible destinations, training costs and opportunity cost. Training stipend is not future consultant salary.
- **After SS / established physician:** Compare a broad physician career with focused specialty posts using real written offers; examples for cardiology cannot be transferred to every DM.

- Strong adult-medicine judgement and a safe, supported acute-care role.
- A deliberate choice between broad physician practice and a subspecialty with additional training and infrastructure needs.
- Case allocation, referral access and hospital privileges in the actual city.
- Is fixed pay linked to ICU duties, 24-hour cover or patient collections?
- For SS, verify hands-on procedures, referral base and backup before prioritising a seat count.

## Paediatrics

MD/DNB Paediatrics supports broad child and adolescent care, newborn care, prevention and development work, or progression into pediatric and selected broader organ-specialty pathways. The exact feeder and award must be checked: a pediatric fellowship and a DM/DrNB with a similar title are different qualifications.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **General pediatrician** — Children's OPD, inpatient hospital, public/community or private practice. Provide evidence-based care for infants, children and adolescents, preventive care, counselling and appropriate referral. This is a clinical endpoint, subject to registration and demonstrated competence. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Paediatrics curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Paediatrics&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Hospital / newborn / emergency pediatric clinician** — Appropriately staffed hospital or pediatric service. The curriculum includes childhood emergencies and care of normal, at-risk and sick newborns. It does not establish that every graduate can independently run any level of PICU/NICU without additional competence and staffing. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Paediatrics curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Paediatrics&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Growth, development, prevention and community child-health clinician** — Community programme, outpatient or multidisciplinary developmental service. Assess growth and neurodevelopment, prevention, rehabilitation and family/social context within the trained scope; focused developmental training is a separate option. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Paediatrics curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Paediatrics&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Senior residency, education or clinical research** — Teaching or specialist children's service. Develop clinical depth, teach and undertake audit/research. Formal faculty recruitment needs the current eligibility and appointment rules. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Paediatrics curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Paediatrics&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)

### Fellowships and focused training

FNB and institutional awards are separate routes; verify current registration, recognition, privileges, intake, fees and stipend. This is an evidenced selection, not an exhaustive national fellowship register.

- **Paediatric Endocrinology — NBEMS-accredited institutions**. FNB. Entry: FET 2025 and NBEMS centralized fellowship counselling. Eligibility: MD/DNB Paediatrics explicitly listed.. Duration: 2 years; confirm specialty curriculum and current allotment. NBEMS FNB, entered through FET rather than NEET-SS. Separate qualification from DM/MCh/DrNB; cited Gazette annexure excludes FNB as a recognized qualification for teaching-faculty purposes. Check current exact award status and curriculum. ### Other pathways

- **Broad pediatric practice with selective referral**. General pediatrics, development, prevention and newborn care are part of the core curriculum. A specialist referral network and appropriate level-of-care limits matter more than presenting every possible course as compulsory. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Paediatrics curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Paediatrics&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)

### Salary and stipend observations

- **Associate Professor (regular/deputation pay scale), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 138,300–209,200 per month; Pay-matrix basic. Stage: Postgraduate academic career: Associate Professor. Qualification: Recognised MD/MS or accepted equivalent in the concerned broad specialty; relevant DM/MCh/equivalent additionally required for superspecialty posts.. Experience: Six years of recognised teaching/research after the qualifying MD/MS/equivalent for broad specialties; three years after a recognised three-year DM/MCh for superspecialty appointments, with other historical routes separately specified.. Level13-A1+ monthly BASIC scale; applicable allowances and NPA are separate and have not been calculated. Scale maximum is a progression endpoint, not an advertised fresh-graduate package or guaranteed offer. ### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

A universal saturated/not-saturated label is not established for paediatrics. Evaluate the location, funded role, skill mix and service model.

**Evidence limits:** Curricula and admissions data are not measured local job demand, hiring competition or take-home income. Added training seats and patient need do not establish funded vacancies or a profitable independent service.

- Separate broad pediatric posts from NICU/PICU or organ-specialty posts requiring specific advanced training and experience.
- Assess births, child/adolescent population, referral catchment, payer mix and existing service capacity; a disease-need story is not a job offer.
- For hospital work, verify night rota, nursing ratios, neonatal/pediatric equipment and access to specialist/transport backup.
- For a focused fellowship, verify personal supervised cases, outcomes review, recognition and how recent graduates were hired.
- Model vaccination, consultation and inpatient receipts separately from salaries and net owner profit.
- Twenty destination specialties are listed for Paediatrics in NEET-SS 2025; some are broader organ-specialty paths, so inspect the intended patient population and curriculum.
- Annexure A and reverse Annexure B disagree on award labels for Paediatric Cardiology and Paediatric Critical Care. This file uses DM/DrNB from B, with the discrepancy preserved; the actual dated matrix decides which seats exist.
- FNB Pediatric Hemato-Oncology is not interchangeable with DM Pediatric Oncology; similarly a one-year PDCC in Pediatric Critical Care is not a three-year DrNB.
- Many institutional fellowships are options for a specific skill gap, not evidence that an MD alone is unusable.

Sources: [NBEMS DNB Paediatrics curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Paediatrics&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5); [CMC 2026-2027 pediatric fellowships](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 35)

**Open evidence gaps**

- A current matrix seat for every listed award is not established merely by feeder eligibility; one award may have no rows while another exists.
- No representative pediatric job density, specialty-specific vacancy rate or net practice income distribution was established.
- Exact faculty-equivalence and international portability of each fellowship need separate current verification.

### Editorial career planning

A broad clinical choice with many eligible later destinations, but it requires comfort with children, families and acute deterioration. I would distinguish general paediatrics, neonatal intensive care and other focused careers early.

- **After MD:** General paediatric/SR employment or continued supervised training; a fresh graduate should examine independent NICU/PICU expectations carefully.
- **Fellowship / SS:** Many distinct destinations exist, with award-specific eligibility and seats. FNB and institutional training remain separate from DM/DrNB.
- **After SS / established practice:** Compare focused jobs with general paediatric continuity practice. A DM Neonatology advert is not a general paediatric salary benchmark.

- Safe general paediatric and emergency care with appropriate senior support.
- A later focus matched to local services and funded demand, rather than a certificate alone.
- NICU/PICU responsibilities, roster burden and the employer's case allocation.
- Who is physically present at night for neonatal and paediatric emergencies?
- Check nursing ratios, equipment, transport/referral systems and duty hours.
- Paediatric Critical Care DM is eligible but absent from the selected matrix; absence is not zero national seats.

## Biochemistry

MD/DNB Biochemistry is a diagnostic, quality, teaching and research pathway rather than merely a classroom subject. The current NEET-SS bulletin explicitly allows Clinical Haematology as well as Medical Genetics; those are substantial additional clinical training decisions, not a consequence of interpreting biochemical tests.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **Clinical-biochemistry laboratory specialist** — Hospital or diagnostic laboratory. Generate, interpret and evaluate chemistry/metabolic/molecular laboratory information and consult with treating clinicians. Actual signatory and service responsibilities require current credentials, privileges and relevant laboratory rules. Sources: [MD Biochemistry revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Biochemistry-revised-Guidelines.pdf) (PDF page(s): 4, 2)
- **Laboratory quality / operations pathway** — Hospital laboratory or independent diagnostic service. The curriculum includes setting up, supervising and managing a diagnostic biochemistry laboratory, quality assurance, error analysis and laboratory information systems. It does not establish a business return or permission to sign every discipline of a combined laboratory. Sources: [MD Biochemistry revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Biochemistry-revised-Guidelines.pdf) (PDF page(s): 4, 2)
- **Senior residency / medical teaching pathway** — Medical college or teaching hospital. Teach biochemical concepts and laboratory interpretation to medical and allied-health learners, build supervised service competence and pursue academic recruitment subject to current eligibility rules. Sources: [MD Biochemistry revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Biochemistry-revised-Guidelines.pdf) (PDF page(s): 4, 2)
- **Metabolic / molecular biomedical researcher** — University, hospital research team or diagnostic-development setting. Develop and validate laboratory methods, plan experiments and analyze data in metabolic and molecular medicine. A research post or PhD needs its own qualification, methods and funding assessment. Sources: [MD Biochemistry revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Biochemistry-revised-Guidelines.pdf) (PDF page(s): 4, 2); [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / phd](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF p.125)

| Award | Specialty | Seats | Entry / feeder notes |
|---|---|---:|---|
| DM | Clinical Haematology | 39 | NEET-SS: Medical Group; MCC SS counselling; Eligibility and seat availability are different; this is the dated initial matrix, not an admission guarantee. |
| DrNB | Clinical Haematology | 50 | NEET-SS: Medical Group; MCC SS counselling; Eligibility and seat availability are different; this is the dated initial matrix, not an admission guarantee. |
| DM | Medical Genetics | 9 | NEET-SS: Medical Group; MCC SS counselling; Eligibility and seat availability are different; this is the dated initial matrix, not an admission guarantee. Any recognised MD/MS/DNB broad specialty is eligible; Medical Group question paper is required. This does not open the other Medical Group destinations. |
| DrNB | Medical Genetics | 4 | NEET-SS: Medical Group; MCC SS counselling; Eligibility and seat availability are different; this is the dated initial matrix, not an admission guarantee. Any recognised MD/MS/DNB broad specialty is eligible; Medical Group question paper is required. This does not open the other Medical Group destinations. |

### Superspecialty route details

- **DM Clinical Haematology** (Verified feeder). NEET-SS 2025; the course-specific question-paper group and MCC counselling apply.. MD/DNB Biochemistry. Verified in NBEMS NEET-SS 2025 Annexures A/B. Eligibility is expressly listed in the current bulletin; do not confuse this clinical DM/DrNB with a short laboratory fellowship. ### Fellowships and focused training

FNB and institutional awards are separate routes; verify current registration, recognition, privileges, intake, fees and stipend. This is an evidenced selection, not an exhaustive national fellowship register.

- **Clinical Molecular Genetics and Genomics — SGPGI, Lucknow**. Post Doctoral Certificate Course (PDCC). Entry: SGPGI 2026 institutional entrance examination and merit-based selection/counselling; current-cycle conditions and future notices must be checked.. Eligibility: MD/MS/DNB Biochemistry is expressly listed, along with Pathology and other named laboratory-based subjects.. Duration: 1 year (2026 brochure programme-duration table, p.4). Institutional skills-development fellowship/certificate; not a DM/MCh/DrNB or a substitute for an NMC-recognized course. Verify current award, accreditation, privileges and employer acceptance. Sources: [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF brochure / biochemistry](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp.4,6,12-16)
- **Clinical Genetics — Christian Medical College Vellore**. Medical postdoctoral fellowship. Entry: Institution-specific medical postdoctoral fellowship selection; read the current course admission bulletin, not NEET-SS.. Eligibility: MD/DNB in any subject (or MS/DNB Obstetrics and Gynaecology), plus all course-specific admission conditions.. Duration: 2 years. Prospectus is common to courses throughout the year and must be read with the course-specific admission bulletin. All listed fellowships are skills-development programmes, not equivalent to or replacements for NMC-recognized courses (p.36). Course tables do not establish a live application window or guaranteed seat; verify the current selection notice. Sources: [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / genetics](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.33,36,46-47)

### Other pathways

- **PhD in Biochemistry / Clinical Biochemistry**. CMC lists both disciplines among PhD programmes. Verify the current university/institute eligibility, supervisor, research focus and funding; the prospectus list is not a guaranteed funded place. Sources: [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / phd](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF p.125)
- **Laboratory method validation and clinician collaboration**. Build a portfolio in analytical validation, quality assurance, data interpretation and clinical consultation; these are core competencies and can matter more than an unrelated short credential. A named diabetes or endocrinology fellowship is not automatically open to Biochemistry. Sources: [MD Biochemistry revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Biochemistry-revised-Guidelines.pdf) (PDF page(s): 4, 2)
- **Patient-safety / quality administration**. SGPGI 2026 Patient Safety including Infection Control PDCC lists any broad specialty among feeders. It is an optional systems qualification with its own selection, not a new broad clinical specialty. Sources: [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF brochure / safety](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp.4,6,12-16)

### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

Evaluate funded academic posts and specialist laboratory work separately from laboratory ownership. Automation in a laboratory does not, by itself, establish either unemployment or guaranteed demand for medical biochemists.

**Evidence limits:** No current, geography-matched workforce supply/demand study or systematic vacancy census was established in this research. Curricula and advertised training programmes show scope and possible differentiation; they cannot measure market saturation, predict income, or guarantee employment.

- Check which local employers recruit MD/DNB Biochemistry for clinical, quality and teaching roles, and what responsibility the position actually carries.
- Ask whether residents interpret abnormal results with clinicians or mainly perform repetitive sample work.
- Verify hands-on access to assay validation, quality management, molecular methods, instrument troubleshooting and laboratory informatics.
- For a proposed laboratory, examine test volume, referral concentration, reagent/service costs, qualified staff and signatory requirements rather than using gross test revenue as salary.
- Select a department with meaningful diagnostic service and quality training alongside teaching.
- Clinical Haematology eligibility is verified, but the work and additional training are very different from routine chemistry.
- Do not treat biochemical knowledge of hormones or diabetes as automatic eligibility for DM Endocrinology.

Sources: [MD Biochemistry revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Biochemistry-revised-Guidelines.pdf) (PDF page(s): 4, 2); [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF brochure / biochemistry](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp.4,6,12-16)

**Open evidence gaps**

- Current INI-SS laboratory-specific courses, programme feeders and seats are not fully audited here.
- No nationally representative employment or saturation study was established.
- No direct FNB course specifically for Biochemistry was established in the FET 2025 Indian candidate list.

### Editorial career planning

Consider this for laboratory medicine, analytical science, teaching and quality work. I would actively test the academic-versus-service job market and available laboratory responsibility before selecting it mainly for lifestyle.

- **After MD:** Academic SR or qualified laboratory role; inspect the actual duties and recruitment rules.
- **Focused training / SS:** Check the specifically verified laboratory/SS feeders and programme eligibility; not every similarly named analytical course is a medical SS.
- **Established career:** Academic promotion, lab quality/service leadership or research. General biochemist salary data includes many non-medical occupations.

- Medical laboratory interpretation, analytical quality and clinical communication.
- A defined academic or service-laboratory role that specifically values a medical qualification.
- Focused laboratory/research capability and leadership, with the current appointment criteria checked.
- Does the salary source specifically require MBBS plus MD/DNB Biochemistry?
- How much clinical interpretation, quality assurance and real laboratory access do trainees receive?
- Industry salary progression was not established by robust MD-specific public data.

## Ophthalmology

MS/DNB Ophthalmology can lead to comprehensive eye care, common surgery and community blindness-prevention work. Retina, cornea, glaucoma, pediatric eye and orbital practice have focused training paths; these should not be confused with NEET-SS DM/MCh awards.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **Comprehensive ophthalmologist / ophthalmic surgeon** — Eye hospital, multispecialty hospital, trust service or private clinic with appropriate operating access. Manage common ophthalmic conditions and surgery within demonstrated skills and privileges; inspect surgical exposure and seek help for work beyond competence. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Ophthalmology curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Ophthalmology&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Community ophthalmology / blindness-control service** — Community programme, outreach-linked hospital or public service. Combine prevention, detection, treatment and rehabilitation; the curriculum explicitly includes blindness control and community epidemiology. Outreach still needs appropriate quality systems and referral support. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Ophthalmology curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Ophthalmology&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)
- **Senior residency, teaching or research** — Academic or high-volume eye service. Consolidate clinical work and contribute teaching, audit or clinical studies. Faculty appointment remains subject to current recruitment requirements rather than a fellowship title alone. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Ophthalmology curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Ophthalmology&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)

### Other pathways

- **Broad comprehensive practice before focused investment**. The curriculum expects competence in common eye problems and recognition of limits. Decide whether a referral-heavy focused service fits the intended setting before buying equipment or pursuing a branded certificate. Sources: [NBEMS DNB Ophthalmology curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Ophthalmology&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5)

### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

A universal saturated/not-saturated label is not established for ophthalmology. Assess the actual paid role and catchment.

**Evidence limits:** Official curricula and admission bulletins establish skills and routes, not current vacancy rates, local competitor capacity, fee realization or net practice income. No representative specialty-by-city workforce dataset was established in this review. More seats or disease burden alone do not prove funded demand.

- Compare actual cataract/medical eye-care demand, payer mix and surgical capacity within the intended catchment.
- Check whether employment supplies theatre, consumables, imaging and trained staff, or expects personal investment.
- For retina/cornea/glaucoma work, establish referral flow, follow-up access and appropriate emergency cover.
- Ask graduates how many supervised operations they personally performed and how complications were taught.
- The verified NEET-SS route here is Medical Genetics via the universal broad-specialty feeder; that does not mean retina has no training route.
- FNB Vitreo Retinal Surgery is a national-board path; CMC and SGPGI institutional fellowships are different awards.
- A course name, machine purchase or high total hospital volume does not establish individual surgical competence or profit.

Sources: [NBEMS DNB Ophthalmology curriculum](https://natboard.edu.in/viewNBEprogrammes?NBE=Curriculum for DNB Ophthalmology&DNB=DNB) (PDF page(s) 5); [CMC 2026-2027 ophthalmology fellowships](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF page(s) 34)

**Open evidence gaps**

- No quantitative local saturation estimate or guaranteed private-practice income was established.
- A generic MD Ophthalmology entry is not silently treated as MS for FNB: the cited FET row says DNB/MS; confirm any equivalent qualification with NBEMS.

### Editorial career planning

Shortlist for a mix of clinic work, precision surgery and a possible focused practice. I would place surgical training quality and verified case access ahead of a university label or a promised fellowship income premium.

- **After MS:** Supported clinical/surgical employment or skill consolidation; verify actual operating exposure.
- **Fellowship:** Compare focus, duration, supervised case targets, fees and stipend. Institutional fellowships are not automatically DM/MCh-equivalent qualifications.
- **Established surgeon:** Compare chain contracts, trust posts or practice ownership using volume, complication support and incentive rules.

- Independent safe common-case surgery and management of complications.
- A focused clinical skill, surgical logbook and referral base matched to local service gaps.
- Chain employment, trust hospitals and ownership offer different case allocation, incentives and capital risk.
- Is the job asking for an independently competent phaco/retina/cornea surgeon rather than a fresh MS?
- Check contract terms and guaranteed fixed pay separately from procedure incentives.

## Radio-diagnosis / Radiology

MD/DNB Radiodiagnosis supports broad diagnostic radiology work without requiring a fellowship for every reporting job. Further training can develop organ-based reporting or procedural expertise; recognized DM/DrNB routes, FET/FNB and institutional fellowships have different admission and award structures.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **Diagnostic radiologist** — Hospital, imaging department or diagnostic service. Conduct and interpret imaging studies across radiography, ultrasound, CT and MRI according to competence and privileges, communicate findings and advise on appropriate investigations. Sources: [MD Radiodiagnosis competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiodiagnosis.pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 1)
- **Emergency / hospital radiology** — Acute hospital or trauma service. Provide imaging input to emergency and trauma care, including timely communication and medico-legal aspects. Contractual night/on-call arrangements and supervision vary by institution. Sources: [MD Radiodiagnosis competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiodiagnosis.pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 1)
- **Image-guided procedural work** — Hospital with appropriate imaging, clinical support and governance. Develop procedural responsibility based on documented training, supervised experience and privileges; the MD curriculum includes interventional knowledge/skills, but a degree alone is not blanket permission for every complex neurovascular or vascular procedure. Sources: [MD Radiodiagnosis competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiodiagnosis.pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 1)
- **Senior residency / academic radiology** — Teaching hospital or medical college. Combine supervised reporting, teaching and research; academic appointment and promotion depend on current eligibility and recruitment rules. Sources: [MD Radiodiagnosis competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiodiagnosis.pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 1)
- **Focused imaging / imaging researcher** — Specialist centre or multidisciplinary imaging team. Build depth in a chosen case mix such as paediatric, cardiovascular, musculoskeletal or cancer imaging. The named institutional programmes below provide examples rather than compulsory credentials for all posts. Sources: [MD Radiodiagnosis competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiodiagnosis.pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 1); [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / radiology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.34-36)

### Other pathways

- **Organ-based reporting development**. Structured rotations, supervised reporting and multidisciplinary feedback can develop a defined imaging interest. Verify the difference between an observership, hands-on fellowship, FNB and recognized DM/DrNB before comparing programmes. Sources: [MD Radiodiagnosis competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiodiagnosis.pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 1); [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / radiology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.34-36); [FET 2025 Information Bulletin: fellowship feeders](https://www.nbe.edu.in/IB/FET%202025%20information%20bulletin%2012.01.2026_v2.pdf) (Annexure B pp.130-131; programme and recognition pp.7-9)
- **Imaging-service ownership or partnership**. Consider this only as a separate service/business model with appropriate facilities and regulatory compliance; employment as a radiologist does not require personally owning CT/MRI equipment. Neither curriculum nor fellowship catalogue establishes investment returns. Sources: [MD Radiodiagnosis competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiodiagnosis.pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 1)

### Salary and stipend observations

- **Assistant Professor (regular/deputation pay scale), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 101,500–167,400 per month; Pay-matrix basic. Stage: Postgraduate academic career: Assistant Professor. Qualification: Recognised MD/MS or accepted equivalent in the concerned broad specialty; relevant DM/MCh/equivalent additionally required for superspecialty posts.. Experience: Three years of recognised teaching/research after the qualifying MD/MS/equivalent for broad specialties; the ad has separate, qualification-dependent rules for superspecialties.. Level12 monthly BASIC scale; applicable allowances and NPA are separate and have not been calculated. Scale maximum is a progression endpoint, not an advertised fresh-graduate package or guaranteed offer. Mapped specialties have a nonzero vacancy at this rank in the dated advertisement; category, selection and full eligibility still apply. Sources: [AIIMS Raebareli faculty recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/FACULTY/2026/467](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C118) (pp2-6 qualifications/experience; p7 pay tables; pp8-9 department/rank vacancies; p15 contractual conditions)
- **Assistant Professor (contractual appointment), AIIMS Raebareli, Raebareli, Uttar Pradesh (Other / smaller city)**: INR 142,506–142,506 per month; Consolidated remuneration. No gross/net/CTC breakdown, modality workload or guarantee-versus-incentive split is supplied. Sources: [Radiologist Consultant in Yunay Consultancy Services LLP at Deoghar - Job Id:1468575](https://www.placementindia.com/job-detail/radiologist-consultant-in-yunay-consultancy-services-llp-at-deoghar-1468575.htm) (Actual job-detail page: pay/experience header, Job Description and Education sections)

### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

Evaluate general reporting, modality expertise and procedural services separately, in the geography where you intend to work. A fellowship catalogue does not establish a shortage, and AI/teleradiology forecasts are not measured evidence of future job loss or security.

**Evidence limits:** No current, geography-matched workforce supply/demand study or systematic vacancy census was established in this research. Curricula and advertised training programmes show scope and possible differentiation; they cannot measure market saturation, predict income, or guarantee employment.

- Collect actual reporting-job offers with modality mix, workload, night duty, turnaround expectations, responsibility and contract status.
- Check how much independent ultrasound/scanning and supervised CT/MRI reporting residents actually perform.
- For interventions, verify operator case logs, supervision, patient selection, ICU/anaesthesia backup, complication management and credentialing.
- For ownership, model referral concentration, utilization, maintenance, staffing, equipment finance and compliance costs separately from personal salary.
- Ask whether a proposed subspecialty fellowship produces independent case competence and a credible local role rather than only a label.
- Prefer programmes with feedback on your own reports and practical modality access.
- The same field name can denote an institutional fellowship, FNB or DM/DrNB; compare admission, duration, training and recognition.
- The FET 2025 table expressly permits MD/DNB Radiodiagnosis for Neurovascular Intervention, even though older generic descriptions may call it postdoctoral.

Sources: [MD Radiodiagnosis competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiodiagnosis.pdf) (PDF page(s): 2, 1); [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / radiology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.34-36); [FET 2025 Information Bulletin: fellowship feeders](https://www.nbe.edu.in/IB/FET%202025%20information%20bulletin%2012.01.2026_v2.pdf) (Annexure B pp.130-131; programme and recognition pp.7-9)

**Open evidence gaps**

- Current INI-SS imaging routes and their institution/session-specific seats are not fully audited in this route file.
- TMC options are conditional catalogue leads until the current admission notice is verified.
- No representative city-level salary, vacancy or saturation census is established by these training documents.

### Editorial career planning

A strong shortlist candidate if you enjoy diagnostic imaging, continuous technical learning and high reporting responsibility. I would judge it by actual imaging exposure, reporting supervision and the type of work you want to do, rather than treating AI predictions as a settled employment outcome.

- **After MD:** Supervised or consultant reporting roles matched to readiness; compare modality mix, workload and night duties.
- **Fellowship / DM/DrNB:** Review eligible interventional/neuroimaging and other verified routes; verify hands-on access and institutional privileges.
- **Established radiologist:** General or focused reporting, department leadership, procedures or ownership. Centre revenue and per-scan fees do not equal personal salary.

- Demonstrable multimodality reporting and ultrasound skills within privileges and applicable law.
- The difference between daytime reporting, emergency/night coverage, remote work and procedures.
- An intervention-focused route may require additional training, procedural volume, beds and multidisciplinary backup.
- How many studies per shift, which modalities, what turnaround time and who supports difficult cases?
- For remote reporting, check employment status, data protection, indemnity, quality review and payment per study.
- Neither strong current ads nor AI forecasts establish a long-term salary floor.

## Dermatology, Venereology & Leprosy

MD/DNB Dermatology, Venereology and Leprosy is already a clinical and procedural specialty. Further medical-dermatology, paediatric-dermatology or dermatosurgery fellowships can deepen a chosen area; a cosmetic certificate is not automatically a recognized superspecialty, and the current NEET-SS table establishes Medical Genetics as the cross-branch DM/DrNB option.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **Clinical dermatologist** — Hospital, ambulatory clinic or appropriately registered private practice. Diagnose and manage skin, mucosal, venereal and leprosy-related disease, including patient counselling and appropriate investigation. Complex care and prescribing still depend on competence, facilities and current requirements. Sources: [MD Dermatology, Venereology and Leprosy competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Dermatology.pdf) (PDF page(s): 1, 5, 2)
- **Procedural dermatology pathway** — Dermatology clinic or hospital with suitable equipment and safeguards. Develop the procedures actually learned and supervised during training, with further dermatosurgical training where needed. Modern procedures, devices and hair/cosmetic services should be matched to demonstrable competence and privileges rather than assumed from the MD alone. Sources: [MD Dermatology, Venereology and Leprosy competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Dermatology.pdf) (PDF page(s): 1, 5, 2); [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / dermatology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.33,36,41-42)
- **Institutional medical / paediatric dermatology** — Hospital or multidisciplinary referral service. Manage complex disease, systemic-treatment monitoring and specialised case mixes; CMC fellowships provide examples of deeper medical and paediatric training. They are not compulsory for every dermatologist job. Sources: [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / dermatology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.33,36,41-42)
- **Public-health / teaching / research pathway** — Medical college, hospital and relevant public programmes. The curriculum covers STD/leprosy programmes, teaching and research alongside clinical practice. Senior residency and faculty jobs require current institutional and regulatory eligibility checks. Sources: [MD Dermatology, Venereology and Leprosy competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Dermatology.pdf) (PDF page(s): 1, 5, 2)

### Other pathways

- **Focused supervised skill development**. Use case-based training, mentorship and audited procedural outcomes to choose additional learning. The CMC dermatosurgery programme includes lasers, skin/nail-related procedures, vitiligo/acne-scar surgery and hair procedures, but its skills-development fellowship is not equivalent to a DM/MCh. Sources: [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / dermatology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.33,36,41-42)
- **Academic clinical-research development**. Build research methods, teaching and medical-dermatology expertise alongside clinical practice. A curriculum or an institutional course does not guarantee an academic vacancy or entitlement to an unrelated surgical qualification. Sources: [MD Dermatology, Venereology and Leprosy competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Dermatology.pdf) (PDF page(s): 1, 5, 2)

### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

Compare medical dermatology, hospital employment, general outpatient practice and cosmetic/procedural businesses separately. Neither popularity of the branch nor a visible number of clinics is a measured saturation result, and cosmetic revenue cannot be treated as personal salary.

**Evidence limits:** No current, geography-matched workforce supply/demand study or systematic vacancy census was established in this research. Curricula and advertised training programmes show scope and possible differentiation; they cannot measure market saturation, predict income, or guarantee employment.

- Collect recent employed dermatologist job offers and distinguish fixed pay, incentives, hours, patient volume and sales targets.
- For a clinic, examine local disease needs, referral sources, patient retention, rent, staffing, device costs and ethical marketing constraints.
- Check exposure to medical dermatology, inpatient consults, paediatric cases and systemic therapies as well as cosmetic equipment.
- For a fellowship, verify supervised operator numbers, complication management, follow-up and the actual awarding/recognizing body.
- Ask whether your proposed niche addresses a local unmet need rather than assuming an extra certificate guarantees demand.
- Choose programmes with broad medical dermatology and supervised procedures; a device list does not prove trainee access.
- CMC Dermatopathology is a Pathology-feeder programme in the current table, so it is not included as an eligible Dermatology fellowship here.
- A short cosmetic/hair course should not be presented as a recognized DM/MCh or universal procedure permission.

Sources: [MD Dermatology, Venereology and Leprosy competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Dermatology.pdf) (PDF page(s): 1, 5, 2); [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / dermatology](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.33,36,41-42)

**Open evidence gaps**

- No direct Dermatology-specific FNB route was established in the FET 2025 Indian candidate list.
- No current systematic nationwide dermatology vacancy/saturation study was established.
- Additional university/society/international fellowships need individual current eligibility and recognition checks; none is inferred from a similar course title.

### Editorial career planning

Consider it for skin disease and outpatient care first. Aesthetic work is an optional business and skill path with its own competition and costs; I would not choose the branch solely on social-media revenue claims.

- **After MD:** Clinical dermatology employment or supported practice; distinguish a dermatologist post from an MBBS cosmetology advert.
- **Focused training:** Check procedural fellowship eligibility and supervision. A pathology-only Dermatopathology fellowship is not available merely because you hold MD Dermatology.
- **Established practice:** A mix of medical dermatology, focused procedures, academia or business ownership according to competence and demand.

- Good clinical dermatology and longitudinal patient care.
- Demonstrated procedural competence, ethical patient selection and realistic case demand.
- Equipment finance, marketing, consumables and employer incentives can materially affect aesthetic business profit.
- What proportion of the role is medical dermatology, sales-linked aesthetics and procedure targets?
- Confirm fixed salary, commissions, equipment costs and clinical autonomy.

## Nuclear Medicine

MD/DNB Nuclear Medicine already trains for diagnostic and therapeutic nuclear-medicine practice, teaching and research. Focused radionuclide-therapy or correlative-imaging training is available, while the current NEET-SS feeder table establishes Medical Genetics rather than an automatic DM in Radiology, Oncology or Endocrinology.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **Diagnostic nuclear-medicine physician** — Nuclear-medicine department with appropriate authorized facilities. Interpret functional/hybrid imaging and integrate it with the clinical question; training covers gamma-camera/SPECT/PET and hybrid systems. Available modalities and reporting responsibility depend on the post and credentials. Sources: [MD Nuclear Medicine revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Nuclear_medicine%20_(%20revised%20).pdf) (PDF page(s): 3, 6, 9)
- **Therapeutic nuclear-medicine physician** — Radionuclide-therapy service within an appropriately equipped and authorized institution. The curriculum includes radionuclide therapy and radiation protection. Specific therapy responsibilities require training, safety systems, facility permissions and privileges; a fellowship is not a substitute for those requirements. Sources: [MD Nuclear Medicine revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Nuclear_medicine%20_(%20revised%20).pdf) (PDF page(s): 3, 6, 9); [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF brochure / nuclear](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp.4,6,12-16)
- **Departmental quality / multidisciplinary service role** — Hospital nuclear-medicine team. Contribute to instrument/radiopharmaceutical quality, safe processes and clinical communication as part of a multidisciplinary service. Medical, physics and technologist responsibilities are distinct; the MD does not automatically confer every radiation-safety designation. Sources: [MD Nuclear Medicine revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Nuclear_medicine%20_(%20revised%20).pdf) (PDF page(s): 3, 6, 9)
- **Senior residency / academic or clinical research pathway** — Teaching hospital, cancer centre or research unit. Develop advanced supervised practice, teach medical/allied-health learners and undertake clinical/basic research. Current appointment and research-programme requirements still apply. Sources: [MD Nuclear Medicine revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Nuclear_medicine%20_(%20revised%20).pdf) (PDF page(s): 3, 6, 9)

### Superspecialty route details

- **DM Medical Genetics** (Verified feeder). NEET-SS 2025; the course-specific question-paper group and MCC counselling apply.. MD/DNB Nuclear Medicine, under the any-other-broad-specialty provision. Verified in NBEMS NEET-SS 2025 Annexures A/B. The current bulletin explicitly permits any MD/MS/DNB broad specialty; this may be a major shift toward clinical genetics rather than a narrow extension of the original branch. Feeder eligibility does not imply a seat in every college or permission to use the same route in an INI-SS institution. Duration: Confirm the current institution prospectus. A current general DM duration rule was not verified in this source set; the NEET-SS feeder table alone is not duration evidence.. MD/DNB Nuclear Medicine, under the any-other-broad-specialty provision. Verified in NBEMS NEET-SS 2025 Annexures A/B. The current bulletin explicitly permits any MD/MS/DNB broad specialty; this may be a major shift toward clinical genetics rather than a narrow extension of the original branch. Feeder eligibility does not imply a seat in every college or permission to use the same route in an INI-SS institution. Duration: 3 years after the eligible broad-specialty qualification; verify the current admitting prospectus and applicable leave/extension rules.. ### Fellowships and focused training

FNB and institutional awards are separate routes; verify current registration, recognition, privileges, intake, fees and stipend. This is an evidenced selection, not an exhaustive national fellowship register.

- **Correlative Imaging — SGPGI, Lucknow**. Post Doctoral Certificate Course (PDCC). Entry: SGPGI 2026 institutional entrance examination and merit-based selection/counselling; current-cycle conditions and future notices must be checked.. Eligibility: MD/DNB Nuclear Medicine only in the cited 2026 table; do not infer eligibility for Radiodiagnosis from a nearby radiology row.. Duration: 1 year (2026 brochure programme-duration table, p.4). Institutional skills-development fellowship/certificate; not a DM/MCh/DrNB or a substitute for an NMC-recognized course. Verify current award, accreditation, privileges and employer acceptance. Sources: [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF brochure / nuclear](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp.4,6,12-16)
- **Radionuclide Therapy — SGPGI, Lucknow**. Post Doctoral Certificate Course (PDCC). Entry: SGPGI 2026 institutional entrance examination and merit-based selection/counselling; current-cycle conditions and future notices must be checked.. Eligibility: MD/DNB Nuclear Medicine.. Duration: 1 year (2026 brochure programme-duration table, p.4). Institutional skills-development fellowship/certificate; not a DM/MCh/DrNB or a substitute for an NMC-recognized course. Verify current award, accreditation, privileges and employer acceptance. Sources: [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF brochure / nuclear](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp.4,6,12-16)
- **Clinical Genetics — Christian Medical College Vellore**. Medical postdoctoral fellowship. Entry: Institution-specific medical postdoctoral fellowship selection; read the current course admission bulletin, not NEET-SS.. Eligibility: MD/DNB in any subject (or MS/DNB Obstetrics and Gynaecology), plus all course-specific admission conditions.. Duration: 2 years. Prospectus is common to courses throughout the year and must be read with the course-specific admission bulletin. All listed fellowships are skills-development programmes, not equivalent to or replacements for NMC-recognized courses (p.36). Course tables do not establish a live application window or guaranteed seat; verify the current selection notice. Sources: [CMC Vellore Prospectus 2026-2027: medical postdoctoral fellowships / genetics](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp.33,36,46-47)
- **Nuclear Theranostics (catalogue option to verify) — Tata Memorial Centre / HBNI**. Certified HBNI fellowship. Entry: Conditional: obtain the current TMC fellowship advertisement before assuming eligibility, intake or application dates.. Eligibility: General catalogue says post-MD/MS/DNB; the current Nuclear Theranostics specialty feeder and selection conditions are not established by that list.. Duration: Catalogue describes 2 years; confirm current advertisement.. Institutional skills-development fellowship/certificate; not a DM/MCh/DrNB or a substitute for an NMC-recognized course. Verify current award, accreditation, privileges and employer acceptance. Sources: [Tata Memorial Centre academics: HBNI certified fellowships](https://tmc.gov.in/academics/) (2 Years Certified HBNI Fellowship list, items8-13 and23)

### Other pathways

- **Therapy / hybrid-imaging expertise within the core branch**. Seek supervised case exposure and further training matched to the actual clinical service. The core curriculum includes diagnostic and therapeutic medicine, so a career does not automatically require moving to a different recognized DM. Sources: [MD Nuclear Medicine revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Nuclear_medicine%20_(%20revised%20).pdf) (PDF page(s): 3, 6, 9); [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF brochure / nuclear](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp.4,6,12-16)
- **Research and multidisciplinary programme development**. NMC includes basic/clinical research, technical quality and managerial skills. Evaluate access to tracers, instrumentation, collaborators and funding for the proposed project rather than assuming every centre can support the same work. Sources: [MD Nuclear Medicine revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Nuclear_medicine%20_(%20revised%20).pdf) (PDF page(s): 3, 6, 9)

Ltd at Rajkot - Job Id:1356524](https://www.placementindia.com/job-detail/nuclear-physician-in-aics-consultancy-service-pvt-ltd-at-surendranagardudhrej-rajkot-1356524.htm) (Actual job-detail page: pay/experience header, Job Description and Education sections)

### Competition, differentiation and shortlisting

Judge the available posts and facilities in your intended region, particularly the actual diagnostic and therapeutic services a centre can sustain. A technology-intensive specialty may have very different training access and employment geography; neither a growth narrative nor a small number of centres establishes saturation.

**Evidence limits:** No current, geography-matched workforce supply/demand study or systematic vacancy census was established in this research. Curricula and advertised training programmes show scope and possible differentiation; they cannot measure market saturation, predict income, or guarantee employment.

- List real nuclear-medicine employers, funded posts and planned services in the locations you would accept.
- Check PET/SPECT/hybrid-imaging exposure, therapy case mix and the balance of hands-on work versus observation.
- Ask about radiopharmaceutical supply, radiation-safety governance, isolation facilities where required and multidisciplinary patient management.
- Clarify whether the offered role includes diagnostic work, therapy responsibility, research, weekends/on-call and any additional mandated qualifications.
- Separate salaried employment from the capital and permission requirements of owning a nuclear-medicine service.
- Verify that a programme offers the treatment and imaging experience you want rather than relying on the branch title.
- SGPGI Correlative Imaging and Radionuclide Therapy are one-year institutional PDCCs in the cited session, not DM/DrNB awards.
- Medical Genetics is a verified cross-branch NEET-SS option; it is a different clinical career direction rather than a named nuclear-medicine superspecialty.

Sources: [MD Nuclear Medicine revised competency-based curriculum](https://nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD_Nuclear_medicine%20_(%20revised%20).pdf) (PDF page(s): 3, 6, 9); [SGPGI 2026 PDCC/PDAF brochure / nuclear](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp.4,6,12-16)

**Open evidence gaps**

- A current specialty-specific TMC Nuclear Theranostics admission notice was not verified.
- No direct Nuclear Medicine-specific FNB route was established in the FET 2025 Indian candidate list.
- INI-SS and other institutional research/fellowship options need a separate current-cycle audit.
- No representative local vacancy or saturation study was established.

### Editorial career planning

A distinct option for someone interested in imaging, physiology, oncology and radionuclide-based care. I would investigate actual centres, training case mix and infrastructure support before interpreting a small seat pool as an employment guarantee.

- **After MD:** Centre-based clinical/service or academic employment with a defined imaging and therapy scope.
- **Focused training / SS:** Verify each advertised nuclear/therapeutic route and feeder; Medical Genetics eligibility does not imply eligibility for every oncology DM.
- **Established career:** Service leadership, academic work or focused clinical practice; infrastructure dependency limits easy assumptions about independent setup.

- Appropriate PET/SPECT and therapeutic exposure under the programme and the employer's privileges.
- A funded position at a functioning centre with reliable equipment and isotope supply.
- A focused service role with multidisciplinary oncology links and current regulatory compliance.
- How many centres would you actually be willing to work in, and do they recruit your qualification?
- Confirm reporting and therapy volumes, radiation-safety arrangements, equipment uptime and backup.
- A historical 2023 salary advert is labelled historical and is not a 2026 market estimate.

## Radiation Oncology

Radiation Oncology (MD Radiotherapy / MD or DNB Radiation Oncology) is a clinical cancer-treatment specialty: assessment, staging, treatment selection, target delineation and plan evaluation, external-beam radiation, brachytherapy, toxicity management and follow-up within a multidisciplinary team. It is distinct from Radio-diagnosis and Nuclear Medicine. A further DM is not an automatic prerequisite for working in the parent specialty. Training quality, supervised clinical responsibility and access to a functioning treatment service matter more than a machine's brand or a university label alone.

### Immediately after MD/MS

- **Senior resident / supervised clinical oncologist** — Government teaching hospital or regional cancer centre. Consolidate assessment, contouring, plan review, on-treatment clinics, acute toxicity management and follow-up. AIIMS Raebareli advertises two Radiotherapy/Radiation Oncology SR posts in its September 2026 notice; AIIMS Bhubaneswar and SGPGI also explicitly list the qualification. These are dated recruitment/training examples, not proof that applications remain open. Sources: [Competency-based postgraduate training programme for MD in Radiotherapy](https://www.nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiotherapy.pdf) (PDF pp1-6: preamble, competencies and practical skills; later curriculum sections); [AIIMS Raebareli ad-hoc Senior Resident recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/SR/2026/532](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C124) (pp1-2 departments and vacancies; p3 qualification, pay and appointment conditions; p4 excluded regular-service benefits); [AIIMS Bhubaneswar Senior Residents (Non-Academic), 4 July 2026](https://aiimsbhubaneswar.nic.in/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/SR_Advt-DFA_revised_approved.pdf) (PDF pp1-2 eligibility/tenure/pay; Annexure I Radiation Oncology row); [SGPGIMS 2026 brochure: Radiotherapy PDCC and senior residency](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp4,7,9-10,12,16)
- **Radiation oncology consultant / associate consultant** — Private cancer hospital or corporate cancer centre. Work in an operational radiation department with defined planning and clinical privileges. Jaipur's consultant advert explicitly asks for MD/DNB Radiation Oncology; other private adverts have broader or inconsistent qualification fields. Confirm actual employer, mentorship, indemnity, fixed pay, clinical workload and independently expected procedures before treating an advert as a suitable fresh-MD role. Sources: [Competency-based postgraduate training programme for MD in Radiotherapy](https://www.nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiotherapy.pdf) (PDF pp1-6: preamble, competencies and practical skills; later curriculum sections); [Consultant Radiation Oncologist advert, Jaipur, Rajasthan (PlacementIndia job 1282673)](https://www.placementindia.com/job-detail/radiation-oncologist-in-recmasters-at-jaipur-1282673.htm) (Job title, salary/experience header, Job Description and qualification fields); [Radiation Oncologist advert, Darbhanga, Bihar (PlacementIndia job 1360224)](https://www.placementindia.com/job-detail/radiation-oncologist-in-swami-vivekanand-cancer-hospital-at-darbhanga-1360224.htm) (Job title, salary/experience header, Job Description and qualification fields)
- **Academic clinician and teacher** — Medical college, university or cancer institute. Combine cancer care, teaching, research and department responsibilities. Faculty entry/promotion depends on the current applicable regulations and the specific advertisement. The August 2026 AIIMS Raebareli matrix contains one Radiation Oncology Associate Professor vacancy; its pay scale and separate contract offer are not fresh-MD salaries. Sources: [Competency-based postgraduate training programme for MD in Radiotherapy](https://www.nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiotherapy.pdf) (PDF pp1-6: preamble, competencies and practical skills; later curriculum sections); [AIIMS Raebareli faculty recruitment, AIIMS/RBL/REC/FACULTY/2026/467](https://aiimsrbl.edu.in/public/recruitments%5Cdownload%5C118) (pp2-6 qualifications/experience; p7 pay tables; pp8-9 department/rank vacancies; p15 contractual conditions)
- **Focused brachytherapy / advanced radiation service work** — Department offering supervised focused training and the relevant clinical service. Build hands-on expertise rather than assume an additional degree is mandatory for every technique. CMC's one-year advanced gynaecologic brachytherapy fellowship covers CT/MRI-guided intracavitary, interstitial and hybrid applications; SGPGI lists a one-year advanced-techniques PDCC. A fellowship is not evidence of a salary increment or universal procedure privileges. Sources: [CMC Vellore course prospectus 2026-27: Advanced Gynaecologic Brachytherapy fellowship](https://www.cmch-vellore.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/14/2026/06/course-prospectus-final-01-06-26.pdf) (PDF pp36 and64; filename version 01-06-26); [SGPGIMS 2026 brochure: Radiotherapy PDCC and senior residency](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp4,7,9-10,12,16)
- **Palliative and supportive oncology** — Cancer centre, palliative oncology team or supervised focused programme. Radiotherapy training includes palliation, symptom support, emergencies and continuity of cancer care. SGPGI explicitly offers a Palliative Oncology PDCC to MD/DNB Radiotherapy. This certificate does not change the degree to MD Palliative Medicine or open unlisted NEET-SS feeders. Sources: [Competency-based postgraduate training programme for MD in Radiotherapy](https://www.nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiotherapy.pdf) (PDF pp1-6: preamble, competencies and practical skills; later curriculum sections); [SGPGIMS 2026 brochure: Radiotherapy PDCC and senior residency](https://sgpgims.org.in/Home/er/2026/12.03.2026_Information%20Brochure%20for%20PDCC_PDAF_SRHS%20etc%202026.pdf) (PDF pp4,7,9-10,12,16)
- **Clinical research and service development** — Academic cancer centre or a funded clinical-research position. The curriculum includes clinical-trial interpretation, research methods, teaching and infrastructure planning. Investigate funded research/PhD posts, trial work or quality/service roles against their own advertisements. The curriculum describes competence; it does not establish a currently funded industry job or salary. Sources: [Competency-based postgraduate training programme for MD in Radiotherapy](https://www.nmc.org.in/storage/new/MD-Radiotherapy.pdf) (PDF pp1-6: preamble, competencies and practical skills; later curriculum sections)

|---|---|---:|---|

| DrNB | Medical Oncology | 200 | NEET-SS: Medical Oncology Group; MCC SS counselling; Eligibility and seat availability are different; this is the dated initial matrix, not an admission guarantee. |

Any recognised MD/MS/DNB broad specialty is eligible; Medical Group question paper is required. This does not open the other Medical Group destinations. |
